All links are built via **`buildSrpHref`/`buildDirectoryQuery`** so they are canonical (params sorted, defaults stripped, spaces as `+`) and never 301. Content is server-rendered into the HTML (crawlable, good internal linking); closed panels carry **`inert`** (out of tab order + AT tree). Interaction: pointer-intent open/close delays, single-open controller, Escape (restoring focus to the trigger), outside-pointerdown, focus-leaving-the-group, and route-change all close; desktop-only (`lg+`), mobile keeps the existing drawer untouched. **Deliberately omitted** (data-honesty): work-mode / employment-type filters (`mode`/`emp` no-op at the ES layer) and interview-questions / salary tools (no such feature).

**Adversarial 4-lens review** (correctness-RSC / a11y / brand-contrast / isolation, each finding independently refuted): **12 raised → 11 confirmed, all fixed.** (MED) pressing Escape while focus was *inside* the open panel reopened it — the focus-restore `.focus()` fired `focusin` → `onFocusCapture` → reopen; guarded with a synchronous `suppressOpen` ref. (LOW) the top comment promised outside-click dismissal that was never implemented — added the `pointerdown` document listener (matching the existing `HeroSearchBar` pattern). (LOW) the presentational panels were being pulled into the client bundle — moved to the slot pattern, which also stops serializing `NavMenuData` as a client prop. (LOW) a hover-close applied `inert` to a panel still holding keyboard focus (dropping focus to `<body>`) — `scheduleClose` now bails if focus is inside the group. (LOW) Escape restored focus to the trigger even when the panel was hover-opened with focus parked elsewhere — restore now only fires when focus was inside. (LOW/NIT ×3) `aria-haspopup="true"` announced a *menu* popup on a `role=region` disclosure — dropped it. (LOW) the featured-employer rating was announced as a bare number — added an `sr-only` "Rating" label. (NIT) `loadHomePageData` ran twice on a home cold render — wrapped it in `React.cache()`. The one refuted finding downgraded a comment-drift claim.

**Gate (integrated, `develop` @ `047eccf`, incl. the `apps/sadmin` merges):** typecheck **12/12** · tests **7/7** (API 637 / web **199** (+11 `nav-hrefs`) / recruiter 32 / sadmin 60 / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19) · build **5/5** apps. **Browser-verified** (real seed, in-app browser via JS probes since screenshots time out): 27 Jobs / 15 Companies links all resolve to canonical routes (roles `?q=backend`, cities `?city=bangalore`, jobs `?industry=it-software` vs companies `?category=it-software`, employers `-overview-<id>`); 4 facets render 4-across; **no horizontal overflow at 1360 or the tight 1024** breakpoint; all text passes AA (tile labels 17.9:1, muted meta / counts 7.79:1, navy count figure 15.4:1, monochrome-navy star 7.83:1); closed panels `inert`; focus-open + single-open (opening one closes the other) + outside-pointerdown-close + Escape-close all verified via dispatched events; 0 console errors. The design mock is an Artifact (private to the owner).

### `feature/sadmin-dashboard-insights` — Super Admin dashboard: pending approvals, new signups, activity trends · 2026-07-29

**The three sections the owner asked for, added to the Super Admin dashboard.** CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **`apps/sadmin` only** — `apps/web`, `apps/recruiter`, `apps/services`, `apps/api` and `packages/*` are **byte-untouched**. **No schema change, no migration, no new feature-flag key, no shared-surface lock.** Two decisions were put to the owner first: charts are **hand-rolled SVG** rather than a new chart dependency (matching the recruiter dashboard and the owner's earlier no-chart-library call), and the job-review *queue* is deferred to its own PR while this one ships the counts.

**Pending approvals.** `CompanyKyc` where `status = PENDING`, and `Job` where `status = PENDING_MODERATION`. The KYC count **deep-links `?status=PENDING`** into the existing admin queue — without the parameter that page resolves to "everything except NOT_SUBMITTED", i.e. PENDING plus already-decided VERIFIED and REJECTED rows, so clicking a "1" would land the reviewer in a longer list to search. The job count is rendered **alongside the moderation flag's state**, because nothing can *enter* `PENDING_MODERATION` while `moderation.jobs.enabled` is off: a bare "0" would read as "all caught up" when the truth is "this queue is not in use". The flag is evaluated through `@jobportal/feature-flags` (a new dependency for this app) rather than an inline `FeatureFlag` row read, per CLAUDE.md §4.

**New signups.** Today / 7 days / 30 days, split candidate vs recruiter, plus a 30-day daily bar chart. ADMIN accounts are excluded — internal staff logins are not signups, and on a small dataset a single seeded admin would visibly distort "today". The headline figures are **derived from the same array the chart plots**, so they can never disagree with the bars beside them.

**Platform activity.** Jobs posted and applications received over 30 days, as a two-series line chart sharing **one Y scale** so the series stay honestly comparable (applications dwarf postings, which is itself the interesting shape).

**Charts.** Geometry lives in `lib/dashboard/chart.ts` — pure, no JSX, no DOM — and is unit-tested for the cases that actually break charts rather than the happy path: a zero axis ceiling (division by zero), a single point (division by `n-1`), a sub-pixel bar rounding away to nothing on a 30-day axis, and degenerate boxes. `TrendChart` renders an `aria-hidden` `<svg>` **plus a visually-hidden `<table>`** of the same numbers — the standard accessible-chart pattern, and better than any `aria-label` summary because the data itself is reachable.

**Timezone.** Every daily bucket is computed in IST via `generate_series` plus the **double** conversion `col AT TIME ZONE 'UTC' AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Kolkata'`. Prisma maps `DateTime` to `timestamp WITHOUT time zone` and these columns hold UTC; on such a column a single `AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Kolkata'` *interprets* rather than converts, and is wrong in **both** directions — proved against this database: a row stored 03:00 UTC lands on the previous day, one stored 19:00 UTC fails to advance to the next. India is UTC+5:30, so the last 5h30m of every Indian day is already "tomorrow" in UTC; bucketing in UTC would misfile every evening signup and application. `generate_series` also returns the window dense and zero-filled in one round trip, which keeps date arithmetic out of JS entirely.

**Adversarial 5-lens review** (SQL correctness · chart+a11y · React/RSC · copy truthfulness · isolation; each finding independently refuted): **27 raised → 8 confirmed (6 distinct), all fixed; 19 refuted.** The **HIGH** one is the reason this review earns its keep: **"Jobs posted" counted unpublished DRAFT jobs, behind a comment asserting the exact opposite.** The comment claimed "drafts have a null postedAt and are excluded by the join" — but `Job.postedAt` is `NOT NULL DEFAULT now()` in the init migration and `create()` never sets it, so a draft gets a `postedAt` at creation. Measured on the dev database: **53 reported where only 52 had ever gone live**. Compounding it, the unit test repeated the same false premise, so the suite asserted the wrong semantics rather than catching the bug. Now filtered `NOT IN ('DRAFT','PENDING_MODERATION')` — deliberately not `status = 'ACTIVE'`, which would erase jobs that genuinely went live and have since expired or closed — and pinned by three new tests. Also fixed: the KYC link's missing filter (above); **`TREND_DAYS` labelled the charts but did not drive the SQL window** (hardcoded `INTERVAL '29 days'` in three places, so editing the constant — exactly what its name invites — would have relabelled every chart while the data stayed at 30 days, with no test failing); the **2px line stroke was clipped in half** at the top and bottom of the viewBox, since `linePoints` maps zero to exactly `y=height` and the peak to exactly `y=0` and an `<svg>` clips at its viewport; **both activity series called `now()` independently**, so a request straddling IST midnight could bucket them onto different day domains while the sr-only table pairs them positionally — both now share one anchor instant passed as a parameter; and a comment cited a `KycStatus.APPROVED` that does not exist (the enum is `VERIFIED`).

**Also carried in from a self-review before those findings landed:** joining on a date **expression** can never use an index, so each dashboard load **sequentially scanned the whole table** (confirmed with `EXPLAIN ANALYZE`: *Seq Scan on Application*). Each join gained a sargable range predicate on the raw column — **4.0ms → 1.6ms** at 371 rows, and the cost is now bounded by the window rather than table size. It sits in the `ON` clause, **not** a `WHERE`: moving it would filter away the generated days with no matching rows, silently turning the LEFT JOIN into an inner join and destroying the zero-filled series.

**Gate (integrated, `develop` @ `77a6ebb` — develop hadn't moved):** typecheck **12/12** · tests **7/7** (API 637 / web 188 / recruiter 32 / **sadmin 17 → 60** / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19) · build **5/5**. **Verified against SQL, not just eyeballed**: KYC pending 1, jobs-to-review 0, jobs posted **52** (after the DRAFT fix), applications 371, axis 30 Jun → 29 Jul. The bar-chart path was exercised by **temporarily backdating six users** — 30 bars, 5 non-zero, heights 45/45/90/45/45 against `niceMax(2)`, and the headline figures read today 1 / 7d 5 / 30d 6 exactly matching SQL — after which the **database was restored byte-identical** (diffed against the captured originals). 0 contrast failures across every new text node (all ≥7.79:1 on the white card, sanity-checked at 21:1), 0 console errors, no horizontal overflow.

**Deferred / follow-ups.** (1) **The job review queue is the next PR** — `moderation.jobs.enabled` remains a trapdoor: it is toggleable from `/admin/feature-flags` but there is still no surface anywhere to approve or reject a `PENDING_MODERATION` job, so turning it on strands every new posting. That PR is also the natural home for moving KYC review out of the old `/admin` console into `/sadmin`. (2) **New signups reads 0 on the local demo database** — the demo seed upserts users by email, so `createdAt` is preserved from the original run and every seeded account sits outside the 30-day window; the chart shows its honest empty state rather than inventing data. Production is unaffected. (3) The trend sections stream behind `<Suspense>`, so with JS disabled the fallback persists — standard Next.js streaming behaviour, and the content is present in the initial HTML.

### `feature/sadmin-portal` — New internal Super Admin portal: login + dashboard KPIs · 2026-07-28

**The platform gains a third portal.** CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). New app **`apps/sadmin`** (Next.js 16, port **3003**, served at the **`/sadmin`** slug), joining `apps/web` (job seeker) and `apps/recruiter`. **`apps/web`, `apps/recruiter`, `apps/services` and every `packages/*` are byte-untouched** (`git diff develop` over each is empty). **No schema change, no migration, no new feature-flag key, no shared-surface lock.** Owner brief: an internal console for reviewing/approving recruiter job posts, processing business verification (KYC) and handling support tickets — starting with a login page and a Dashboard of KPI cards.

**Four decisions taken with the owner before any code was written.** *(1) The `/sadmin` slug* — in this monorepo `apps/recruiter` serves its routes at the ROOT of its own origin, so "slug `/sadmin`" had three possible readings. Chosen: a separate app with Next.js **`basePath: '/sadmin'`**, which delivers full app isolation *and* a literal `/sadmin` prefix on every URL including assets, letting a CDN proxy `careerqueue.in/sadmin/*` with **no path rewriting**. The prefix appears on exactly **one line of `next.config.ts`** and nowhere in application code, so switching to a bare subdomain later is a one-line deletion. *(2) Role model* — reuses the existing **`UserRole.ADMIN`**; adding a `SUPER_ADMIN` enum value would have required the `schema.prisma` lock, a migration, and an audit of every `requireAdmin()`/`AdminGuard` call site for a portal that is a *name*, not a new privilege tier. *(3) KPI #1* — "recruiters/employers" are two different numbers here (10 active recruiter accounts vs 14 companies); the card counts **recruiters**, and says so. *(4) The pre-existing `/admin` console inside `apps/web` **coexists**, untouched — `AdminGuard` is origin-agnostic and cookie-or-Bearer based, so migrating those pages here later is a pure frontend move with zero API changes.

**Design is the recruiter shell, verbatim** (which in turn mirrors the seeker dashboard): a fixed 256px brand-navy rail (**measured `#192249`**, no border), a sticky white top bar, a muted canvas so cards read as elevated, an 18px nav icon in **brand cyan on the active row only**, and a cyan avatar chip + sign-out in the account row. Two traps the recruiter re-skin documented were carried over deliberately: the `Logo` needs **`onDark`** (this app mounts no `ThemeProvider`, so `dark:` utilities are inert and the auto path would put the *navy* logo on the *navy* rail — verified: exactly one `<img>` renders on the rail, the white asset), and every rail control overrides the focus ring to white, because `theme.css` paints `:focus-visible` with `--color-ring` which measures only **1.96:1** on navy — below the 3:1 WCAG 1.4.11 floor — and sets an *author* outline, suppressing the browser's adaptive fallback. **Measured 15.37:1** after the override.

**Three `basePath` traps, all of which fail silently rather than erroring.** `redirect()` must use `nextUrl.clone()` (`new URL(…, request.url)` drops the prefix and loops). The middleware matcher sees paths **already stripped** — but the catch-all compiles to a pattern requiring a literal `/` after `/sadmin`, so it **never matched the bare root** and the portal's own entry URL shipped without the `X-Robots-Tag` the file exists to set (found in review; `/` is now listed separately, verified on all three URLs). And **`next/image` does not prefix a string `src`**: the browser requested `/brand/x.png` while the asset is served only at `/sadmin/brand/x.png`, and enabling the optimizer does not help because its own `url=` query param is un-prefixed too, producing *"The requested resource isn't a valid image … received null"*. Fixed with **static imports** (whose `/_next/*` URL *is* prefixed) plus `unoptimized` — so no application code writes the prefix.

**Auth.** There is exactly one password-login endpoint in the repo and **it performs no role check by design** (the seeker site and recruiter portal share it), so a candidate submitting the admin form would have received a perfectly valid session on the admin origin. New **`POST /auth/admin/login`** verifies the password **first** and only then rejects a non-`ADMIN`, with a message byte-identical to a wrong password — checking role first would answer *"is this address an admin?"* for anyone who can send a request. The credential check was extracted into a shared `verifyCredentials()` so the dummy-hash timing defence and the OAuth-only rejection cannot drift between the two endpoints. **Verified: the same candidate credential returns 200 on `/auth/login` and 401 on `/auth/admin/login`** — proving the rejection is the role gate, not a bad password. Layer 2 is `requireSuperAdmin()` in the `(authed)` layout, and its role check is load-bearing: the `access_token` cookie is shared by all portals on `localhost` (and across subdomains in prod), so a signed-in recruiter arrives already holding a valid token. It **redirects** rather than `notFound()`s — `apps/web`'s `requireAdmin()` 404s to hide `/admin` inside the public seeker app, reasoning that does not transfer to a dedicated origin with its own sign-in page.

**The KPI queries are the substance, and both obvious versions are wrong — in opposite directions.** Recruiters come from `prisma.recruiter.count({ where: { deactivatedAt: null } })`: removing a teammate is a **soft delete** that sets `Recruiter.deactivatedAt` and never touches the `User` row, so `user.count({role:'RECRUITER'})` would keep counting people who can no longer sign in, and could only ever grow. Seekers come from `user.count({ where: { role: 'CANDIDATE' } })`: the `Candidate` profile row is provisioned **lazily** on first `/profile` read, so counting `Candidate` rows under-counts real registrations. "Open" is the label the recruiter UI gives `JobStatus.ACTIVE`. All three `where` clauses are pinned by unit tests rather than left to a comment.

**The seeded super admin.** `packages/db/prisma/seed/admin.ts`, called from the **reference** seed (`pnpm db:seed` — the root-aliased command every developer already runs), not the demo overlay, which is neither root-aliased nor runnable against a non-local `DATABASE_URL`. `passwordHash` is written in **both** `create` and `update` so all three machines converge on the documented credential (the demo seeders set it create-only, which would have let a pre-existing row drift forever). argon2 is called directly with `demo.ts`'s exact parameters because importing `hashPassword` from `@jobportal/auth` would be a **circular workspace dependency**. This account is the **first `ADMIN` this repo has ever had** — a fresh clone previously had zero, making the existing `/admin` console unreachable by anyone.

**Adversarial 5-lens review** (security · basePath-correctness · isolation · a11y/design-fidelity · copy-truthfulness; every finding independently refuted): **31 raised → 20 confirmed (11 distinct), all fixed; 11 refuted.** **Two HIGH, both in the seed, both would have shipped broken and both defeated the feature's own purpose.** First: **`??` never fires on an empty string.** `.env.example` ships `SADMIN_SEED_EMAIL=""` / `SADMIN_SEED_PASSWORD=""` and every developer copies that file, so those arrive as empty strings, not `undefined` — the documented account would never have been created; instead the seed would have planted `User{ email: '', passwordHash: argon2(''), role: 'ADMIN' }`, and the production guard would have compared `''` against the default and **never fired**. Browser verification missed it because the local `.env` predated the new keys, so they were genuinely `undefined` there. Second: **reading env at module level ignored `.env` entirely** — `seed.ts` runs `dotenv config()` *between* its imports, so this module was evaluated **before** the load, and a `SADMIN_SEED_PASSWORD` set in `.env` was silently discarded (verified empirically: the account stayed on the default). Env is now read inside the function; re-verified across a five-case matrix (local+default **seeds**; remote+default **skips**; `NODE_ENV=production` **skips**; remote *or* production with an explicit password **seeds**). Relatedly, the production guard keyed on `NODE_ENV` alone, which nothing in `pnpm db:seed` sets and `.env.example` never defines — it now mirrors `seed-demo.ts`'s defence in depth (`NODE_ENV` **and** a `DATABASE_URL` locality test). Also fixed: the upsert **promoted a pre-existing row to ADMIN without revoking its sessions** (registration is public and issues a 30-day refresh token whose claims are rebuilt from the live user row, so whoever registered the address first would silently inherit an admin session — sessions are now revoked on every seed); **Sentry was wired into `next.config` and `global-error` but never initialised**, so every crash in the highest-privilege portal was dropped while appearing instrumented (all four init files added); the dashboard never set **`data-wide`**, rendering KPI cards at 232px against the recruiter portal's 317px, contradicting the "same design" brief (**now measured 317px**); the loading skeleton's `role="status"` had **no text content** so it announced nothing (a live region is read from its content, not its `aria-label`); the 429 copy promised "wait a minute" for what can be a **10-attempts-per-hour** per-email lockout, and mislabelled the 5/min **per-IP** throttle as per-email; and a `next.config.ts` comment claimed `next/image` srcs *are* basePath-prefixed — the exact opposite of what `Logo.tsx` works around.

**Gate (integrated, `develop` @ `e1a6b33` — develop hadn't moved):** typecheck **12/12** · tests **7/7** (API **637** (+8) / web 188 / recruiter 32 / **sadmin 17 — new workspace** / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19) · build **5/5** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, seeded admin, real data): KPI values **10 / 20 / 52 cross-checked directly against Postgres**; the eye toggle flips `password`→`text` with `aria-label`/`aria-pressed` updating and `type="button"` so it can never submit; anon → `/sadmin/login?next=%2Fdashboard`, **a signed-in recruiter → `/sadmin/login?denied=1`**, admin → 200 with `X-Robots-Tag`; rail `#192249` at 256px with only the white logo; exactly one `aria-current`; **0 console errors**; no horizontal overflow at 1280/768/375.

**Deferred / follow-ups.** (1) **No mobile navigation below 768px** — the rail is `hidden md:flex`, inherited verbatim from the recruiter portal, so at 375px the dashboard renders but there is no way to navigate or sign out. Desktop-only is defensible for internal staff tooling, and fixing it properly is a change to *both* portals. (2) **`moderation.jobs.enabled` is a live trapdoor** — the flag is seeded and toggleable from the existing `/admin/feature-flags` UI, but **no review surface exists anywhere in the codebase**, so turning it on today sends every newly published job to `PENDING_MODERATION` where it can never be approved. That is precisely the "review and approve job posts" feature this portal is for, and it is the natural next PR. (3) **Cookie collision** — signing into sadmin replaces an active recruiter session in the same browser and vice versa (pre-existing between the seeker and recruiter portals); giving admin its own cookie name means editing `packages/auth`, a shared surface, so it was deliberately not folded in. (4) `.claude/launch.json` is **gitignored**, so the sadmin dev-server entry is local-only — teammates run `pnpm --filter @jobportal/sadmin dev`.

### `feature/recruiter-brand-theme` — Recruiter portal re-skinned onto the seeker dashboard's colour theme · 2026-07-26

**The recruiter portal now uses the same colour system as the job-seeker dashboard.** CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **`apps/recruiter` only** — `apps/web` (job seeker), `apps/services`, `apps/api` and `packages/*` are **byte-untouched** (`git diff develop` over each is empty). **No schema change, no migration, no API endpoint, no feature flag, no shared-surface lock.** Owner brief: study how colour is used in the seeker profile/dashboard and apply the same theme to the recruiter portal, touching **no functionality**.

**The finding that shaped the whole approach.** Both apps already `@import "@jobportal/ui/styles/theme.css"` at line 2 of their `globals.css`, so brand navy `--color-primary-600` (#192249), brand cyan `--color-accent-500` (#22A0DA) and the full surface ramp were **already defined and already imported** in the recruiter app — it just never consumed them (**zero `--color-accent-*` occurrences in the entire recruiter source** before this). The job was therefore *"start using the theme you already have"*, not *"add a theme"* — which is why it needed **no edit to `theme.css` or `packages/ui`**, and hence could not affect the seeker site. Every shared atom the recruiter imports (`Button`, `Card`, `Badge`, `Input`, `Select`, `Dialog`, `Skeleton`, `EmptyState`) is also rendered by `apps/web`, so all overrides were applied at the **call site** via the trailing `className` (tailwind-merge lets the caller win) — the technique `JobStatusBadge` already documents for its `fgClass`.

**The shell** (mirroring `apps/web/components/dashboard/DashboardChrome.tsx`): canvas `--color-bg` → `--color-bg-muted` so cards read as elevated (measured separation 11/10/8 per channel); sidebar → solid `--color-primary-600` with `border-r` dropped and 240 → 256px; top bar → white `--color-bg-elevated`; nav rows → alpha-white (`white/70` idle, `bg-white/10` + white active); the nav gains **18px icons with the active one in brand cyan** — the seeker rail's single accent; three section eyebrows (**Hiring / Company / Account**) that only **partition the existing order**, moving/renaming/regrouping nothing; and an account row with a **cyan avatar chip + name + email + icon sign-out**. Every href, label, order, `isActive()` rule, disclosure state and `aria-current` target is behaviourally identical to `develop`.

**Two changes the navy rail *required*** (not cosmetic, and both would have shipped broken without it): `components/brand/Logo.tsx` gained an **`onDark`** prop, because its auto path picks the white asset via `dark:block` and **this app mounts no `ThemeProvider`**, so `[data-theme="dark"]` never matches — it would have rendered the **navy logo on the navy rail**; and `SignOutButton` stopped using the shared `@jobportal/ui` ghost `Button`, whose `text-[var(--color-fg)]` is near-black and invisible on navy (the atom is shared with `apps/web`, so it could not be re-coloured). Behaviour of both is unchanged.

**Consequences of the canvas move, all swept and fixed:** 21 bordered panels and 12 dashed empty states carried **no background fill** and would have rendered *darker than* the white cards beside them; 7 `bg-muted` fills that sit directly on the canvas would have become invisible; on-canvas controls that hovered to `bg-muted` would have hovered to their own background (now lift to `bg-elevated`, the seeker's own pattern). The four near-black `bg-[var(--color-fg)]` selection states (`ApplicantsFilterTabs`, `ApplicantsSortToggle`, `JobsPagination`, `FaqPanel`) became brand navy. Also fixed **`bg-[var(--color-surface-subtle)]`** in `PostJobWizard` + `PostJobFlow`: that token **is not defined anywhere in `theme.css`**, so both panels had been rendering with no fill at all.

**One deliberate deviation from the seeker**, documented in-code: table row hover stays `bg-muted` instead of the seeker's `bg`. Measured against the now-white card, `bg` is only a ~2% delta (6/5/4 per channel) — fine for the seeker's handful of large card rows, too weak for a 50-row recruiter table. `bg-muted` cannot collide with anything because rows sit on the card, not the canvas.

**Adversarial 4-lens review** (behavioural-equivalence · a11y · isolation · missed-surfaces; every finding independently refuted): **12 raised → 6 confirmed (5 distinct), all fixed; 6 refuted.** The headline was a genuine regression the re-skin introduced and that a purely visual check could not catch: `theme.css` paints `:focus-visible` with `--color-ring` (= `--color-primary-500`), which was ~7.5:1 while the rail was white but measures **1.96:1 against `--color-primary-600`** — below the 3:1 WCAG 1.4.11 floor, across the *entire primary navigation* — and because `theme.css` sets an **author `outline`**, the browser's own adaptive focus ring is suppressed, so there was no fallback. Re-pointed to white on the rail's own controls: **1.96:1 → 15.37:1**. Next: `Badge`'s `neutral` variant fills with `--color-bg-muted`, which is now exactly the canvas, so **all 60 Post-a-Job skill chips rendered as bare text** and the neutral status pills in the `/kyc`, ticket-detail, billing and job-detail headers lost their pill — fixed with a new `NEUTRAL_ON_ANY_SURFACE` (elevated fill + hairline ring) applied at five call sites, kept in a JSX-free module so the client wizard pulls no component tree into its bundle. Also: the `/dashboard` route-level **`loading.tsx` skeleton opened with a blank header** (the shared `Skeleton` is also `bg-muted`, and three of its bars sit outside the card); and `FaqPanel`'s empty state was the one dashed panel the scripted sweep missed, because it uses the shared `EmptyState`, which has no fill of its own. Refuted and left alone: the widened Prisma select does **not** add a query (it is a relation on the shell's existing `findUnique`), the section eyebrows are correctly inert, and the inactive filter pills are fine against the canvas.

**Gate (integrated, `develop` @ `c42710a` — develop hadn't moved):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **6/6** (API 629 / web 188 / recruiter 32 / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19 — **every baseline unchanged**, the expected result for a visual-only change) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, demo recruiter Priya @ Nimbus, real data): rail computed `rgb(25,34,73)` at 256px with `border-right: 0px`; white top bar; **only one `<img>` in the rail, the white asset**; cyan active-nav icon and "PS" avatar; exactly one `aria-current` per page; 9/9 authed routes 200 with the new shell and **no leftover undefined token**; 0 console errors; no horizontal overflow at 375. Post-fix: focus ring white at **15.37:1** on both nav rows and sign-out, `/kyc` neutral badges white-with-ring on the canvas, all 60 chips visible, skeleton bars at a 17/17/16 delta. **Contrast sweep over every text node: 14 failures, all one PRE-EXISTING issue** — the Jobs table's `fg-subtle` "Expires / date" line at **2.58:1** (it measured ~2.52:1 *before* this change) — and **zero new failures**.

**Deferred / follow-ups (both pre-existing, deliberately not folded in):** (1) ~**37 `fg-subtle` usages** across the portal need a per-site AA pass — many are decorative (icons, chevrons, em-dashes) and don't need 4.5:1, so doing it properly needs judgement per site and its own verification pass; it is not a side effect of this change. (2) `/jobs` **already scrolls horizontally at exactly 768px on `develop`** (428px of it, caused by the table's `min-w-[880px]` escaping the `overflow-x-clip`); the 256px rail adds **16px** to that existing overflow. Neither was introduced here. Also unchanged and out of scope: the recruiter portal still has **no mobile navigation** below 768px (the rail is `hidden md:flex`, as on `develop`), and the `(auth)` login/register layout was intentionally left on the old flat surface since the seeker dashboard reference does not cover it.

### `feature/recruiter-dashboard-insights` — Recruiter Dashboard: verification progress + KPIs/insights · 2026-07-26

**The recruiter Dashboard, rebuilt from an empty-state stub into the portal's activity snapshot** (SRS §4.9). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **`apps/recruiter` only** — `apps/web` (job-seeker), `apps/services`, `apps/api` and `packages/*` are **byte-untouched** (`git diff develop…HEAD` over each is empty). **No schema change, no migration, no API endpoint, no new feature-flag key, no shared-surface lock.** Display-only: every number is read straight from Postgres in the RSC per the reads/writes split.

**Verification progress is the page lede** (explicit owner requirement: it must come before the metrics). Three axes, all from columns that already exist — no invented schema field, no stored percentage:
- **Work email** — `Recruiter.workEmailVerified`. The only axis that gates anything today (`recruiter-jobs.service.ts` refuses to post without it). There is still no in-app resend endpoint, so the step names the inbox rather than linking a route that does not exist.
- **Company profile** — the eight nullable columns `/profile` already edits (`description`, `logoUrl`, `websiteUrl`, `companyType`, `industryId`, `headquartersCityId`, `employeeCount`, `foundedYear`). Presence only; content is never judged.
- **Company KYC** — `CompanyKyc.status` plus the **exact five submit predicates the API itself enforces** in `recruiter-kyc.service.ts`, mirrored field-for-field and word-for-word so the dashboard can never tell a recruiter they are ready to submit when `submitKyc()` would reject them (a unit test pins that wording). `PENDING` counts as finished from the recruiter's side but **never claims "verified"**; `REJECTED` surfaces the reviewer's reason; `NOT_SUBMITTED` lists what is still needed.

The card **absorbs the standalone `VerifyWorkEmailBanner`** (deleted — step 1 said the same thing, so an unverified recruiter read the identical prompt twice), and **collapses to a single quiet line** once everything is genuinely approved rather than nagging (CLAUDE.md §2). When `killswitch.recruiter_kyc` is ON the KYC step is **dropped entirely** — no dead `/kyc` link, no progress held against a switched-off feature, and the all-clear summary is **assembled from the steps that actually ran** so it cannot claim an approval that never happened. **The three KYC identifiers collapse to booleans in the query layer**, so no GSTIN / PAN / legal name ever enters the returned object or the render tree.

**KPIs and insights** (company-scoped via `Job.companyId` — `postedById` is nullable with `onDelete: SetNull`, so scoping by poster would silently drop jobs whose author left; "posted by you" is surfaced separately): six tiles — jobs posted · open · draft · applications received · needs review · **candidates hired** — plus a **candidate pipeline** (Applied → In review → Shortlisted → Interviewed → Offered → Hired), **jobs by status** deep-linking `/jobs?status=…`, a **needs-attention** list (first-review backlog, expiring within 14 days, open jobs with no applicants, unpublished drafts) and **top jobs by application volume**. Only the viewer's own rows link into `/jobs/[id]`, which 404s for non-owners — the same conservative line the Jobs list draws on teammate rows. Visuals are hand-rolled token bars; **there is no chart library in the repo and one was deliberately not added** (owner confirmed).

**Deliberately omitted rather than fabricated**: job views / impressions / CTR (**no view, impression or event table exists anywhere in the schema**), time-to-hire (`Application.statusHistory` is unpopulated on historical rows), saved-job interest (zero `SavedJob` rows), hire-rate % (1 of 105 on real data reads as broken), post quota (a per-**user** Redis counter needing an extra network hop) and current plan (resolves to "Free" for every company).

**Performance** — the owner flagged that this is the first page after sign-in. The work is split by cost rather than run as one blocking batch: verification needs **one** query and is awaited with the shell; the **six** aggregate queries stream in behind `<Suspense>` with a skeleton, plus a route-level **`loading.tsx` (the first in the repo)**. `React cache()` de-dupes the recruiter→company lookup the layout and the page each paid independently. Two `groupBy` calls collapse thirteen counts into two round-trips, and the Jobs list's per-job skill-match fan-out is deliberately not replicated (it would be an N+1 here). **Measured locally on the real stream: shell 167ms → verification card 261ms → KPIs 321ms**, against 322ms for a fully blocking render. No new dependency: `apps/recruiter` has no Redis access and the repo has no `unstable_cache`/PPR precedent, so neither was introduced.

**Also in this PR (both owner-approved, both pre-existing):** the top-bar `KycStatusBadge` rendered unconditionally while `/kyc` 404s on its killswitch, so a recruiter saw a "Not started"/"Action needed" status for a feature that had been switched off with nowhere to resolve it — now L2-gated. And the `(authed)` shell ran **four mutually independent awaits in series** before returning any JSX; they now run in one `Promise.all`, so the shell costs the slowest rather than the sum on every authed page.

**First test harness in `apps/recruiter`.** The app had no `vitest.config.ts`, no `test` script and zero test files, so `pnpm test` silently skipped the workspace and every prior recruiter PR shipped unverified logic. Added vitest scoped to `lib/**`, mirroring `apps/web`'s config — **32 tests** over the verification computation. Component/RSC rendering stays out of scope (needs jsdom + a React testing stack); those surfaces are browser-verified instead.

**Adversarial review** (Workflow, 4 lenses — correctness/RSC · a11y · isolation/security · copy truthfulness — each finding independently refuted): **33 raised → 17 confirmed, all fixed; 16 refuted.** (HIGH-impact) a step can reach `done === total` while still being unfinished in **two** real states — a KYC filled in but never submitted, and one submitted then **REJECTED** (a rejection writes only status + reason; it clears no field and deletes no document) — so both were credited in full, driving the bar to **100% and announcing "Verification 100% complete" to screen readers beside a "2 of 3 complete" headline on an account that had just been turned down**. Fixed by routing every step's share through one central rule that credits in full only for DONE/IN_REVIEW, plus an invariant test (`percent === 100 ⇔ complete`) across every state — **that invariant then caught the second instance itself**, which the review had not spotted. (MED) the raw GSTIN left the memoised accessor under a comment claiming it did not → identifiers now reduce to booleans at the boundary. (MED) copy promised a verified badge **"on your job postings"** when no such badge exists (it is on the company profile), labelled a count of *applications* as **"Total applicants"**, and asserted an unrecorded read-state ("Not opened yet"). (LOW) two comments claimed nothing flips jobs to `EXPIRED` — **`apps/api/src/job-lifecycle` sweeps them daily** — so Expired and Closed now always render instead of being hidden as permanently empty. (MED, a11y) `first:pt-0 last:pb-0` sat on a link that is the sole child of its `<li>` and therefore always matches both, zeroing every attention-row's padding — **the same bug that flattened the seeker rail rows**; `KpiTile`'s `aria-label` replaced the visible label instead of containing it (WCAG 2.5.3 Label in Name) and silently dropped every hint; `TopJobs` dropped the status badge from its `aria-label`, announcing status on teammates' unlinked rows but not your own; a bar `<div>` sat between `<dt>` and `<dd>`, which `<dl>` does not permit; the skeleton's `role="status"` had only `aria-hidden` descendants and so announced nothing; sr-only state read "Not started" on a row also announcing "5 of 8".

**Gate (integrated, `develop` @ f29b63b — develop hadn't moved):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **6/6** tasks (API 629 / web 188 / **recruiter 32 — new workspace** / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, demo recruiter Priya @ Nimbus, real data): every count cross-checked against Postgres (7 jobs / 7 ACTIVE / 0 DRAFT, 105 applications across all eight statuses, 1 hired, 102 in pipeline); all four KYC states plus the killswitch exercised end-to-end; the rejected-with-everything-on-file case re-verified at **93%** after the fix; **0 contrast failures across every text node in `<main>`**; no horizontal overflow at 1280/768/375; heading order H1→H2 with no skips; 0 console errors. **DB fully restored** — temporary `CompanyKyc` row + 2 `KycDocument` rows deleted, killswitch back OFF, Priya's 7 ACTIVE jobs and all 371 applications intact.

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** views/impressions and time-to-hire need new schema + a write path before they can ship non-fabricated — a separate PR under the DB lock if the owner wants them. `TopJob.isOwn` links only the viewer's own jobs, so a **collaborator** sees their row as plain text (under-linked, never a dead click) — resolving collaborator rows would cost another round-trip. The sidebar's "Verification" item still renders while its killswitch 404s the page; that is a **documented, deliberate convention** (`SidebarNav.tsx` says so explicitly, citing the Verification/Users entries), so it was left alone rather than silently changed. `apps/recruiter/next.config.ts` still lacks `serverExternalPackages` and omits `@jobportal/feature-flags` from `transpilePackages` (both present in `apps/web`); pre-existing and currently working — raised, not changed.

### `feature/career-advice-editorial-v3` — Career Advice hub → all-light editorial redesign v3 · 2026-07-22

**Career Advice hub (`/career-advice`) redesign — v3, and the one that shipped for real** (SRS §4.8). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **`apps/web` only** — `packages/*` + other apps + `theme.css` byte-untouched; **no schema/migration/feature-flag/API/routing/shared-surface-lock** (the career-advice barrel + `globals.css` edits are append-only). **Replaces the rejected v2** (`feature/career-advice-editorial`, which had been the design on `develop` until this merge): the owner rejected **both** the v1 "focused editorial index" and the v2 "editorial-magazine / navy Index-Plate" designs as **"too simple / 3rd-class / poor design."** Root cause of those blind iterations: the preview screenshot/region-zoom **times out** on the tall dev page, so the design was being judged without actually being seen. The fix that worked: a **full-fidelity Artifact** (real desktop width, exact brand hexes, real content, inline-SVG stand-ins for photos since the Artifact CSP blocks external images) pitched for **direction approval before** rebuilding live. The owner approved this direction with two hard rules: **(a) purely light** — absolutely no dark section/card backgrounds in the light theme (navy/cyan only as ink, marks, one button, and the active topic-pill fill); **(b) cover images on every blog** — and keep the current `SiteShell` navbar/footer (not a mock top bar).

**What shipped (all-light editorial magazine):** `SiteShell` → **`CareerMasthead`** (kicker + oversized **serif** headline "Get hired, on purpose." with "purpose" set in `accent-700` italic + `ArticleSearch` + the topic pills serving as the topic nav) → a featured **`CoverTile size="cover" headingLevel={2}`** (horizontal: cover image + serif copy) → a "More from the desk" 2-up **`CoverTile size="tile"`** grid → **`CareerColophon`** (a **light cyan-tint** CTA card — not a dark band). New **`ArticleCover`** renders a real `coverImageUrl` via `next/image` when present, else a **designed on-brand SVG cover** keyed to `tags[0]` (salary / resume / portfolio / applying / early-career + a fallback — flat navy/cyan shapes, token-var fills, `aria-hidden` decorative). The serif is a new scoped **`.font-editorial`** rule in `apps/web/app/globals.css` kept **outside `@layer utilities`** (Tailwind 4 tree-shakes custom classes declared *inside* that layer — a real bug hit during the build; it also needed a clean dev restart to apply). Retired the v2 **`ContentsRail`** + **`article-visuals.ts`** (the old navy-plate system); the shared **`ArticleCard` is untouched** (the homepage articles section can't regress). The `?q=` search + `?tag=` filter + featured/empty-state (`gridArticles.length===0 && !featured`) + archive logic are all preserved. Net diff: 322 insertions / 464 deletions across 10 files (a net simplification — one new component, two retired).

**Data reality (unchanged, no fabrication):** 3 published articles, **all `coverImageUrl=NULL`** → the designed SVG covers render (real photographs would need uploads/seed + a `next.config` host allowlist; designed covers were the deliberate choice, disclosed to the owner). 1 author ("JobPortal Editorial"), ~5 tags. Omitted for lack of a backing model: newsletter, popular-authors, roadmaps, learning-collections, career-guides, free-resources, related-services, most-read/trending/view-counts, bookmark.

**Adversarial 4-lens review** (correctness/RSC · a11y · brand-contrast · isolation — each finding independently refuted): **0 confirmed findings.** No test files changed (apps/web only, `params.ts` untouched → web stays **188**). **Gate (integrated on `develop` @ `0778b20`):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API 629 / web 188 / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, real data): **0 dark section backgrounds** in the light theme; the `.font-editorial` serif applies; the designed SVG covers render for all 3 tag-keyed articles; all text passes AA (kicker/tags `accent-700` 5.58–5.84:1, titles/CTA/active-pill 15–18:1, colophon on the cyan tint 7.29–16.75:1); no horizontal overflow at 1360 / 768 / 375; the topic pills filter correctly; the homepage `ArticleCard` is unaffected.

### `feature/career-advice-editorial` — Career Advice hub → editorial-magazine redesign v2 · 2026-07-20

**Career Advice hub (`/career-advice`) redesign — v2** (SRS §4.8). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **`apps/web` only** — `packages/*` + other apps + `theme.css`/`globals.css` byte-untouched; **no schema/migration/feature-flag/API/routing/shared-surface-lock** (career-advice barrel edit is append-only). The owner rejected the v1 "focused editorial index" (shipped earlier same run) as **"too simple and very poor in design"** and specifically disliked that the blog cards show **no featured images** — while the data reality is unchanged (3 published articles, **zero cover images**, one author, ~5 tags; no fabricated photos allowed).

**Design process:** a **judge-panel Workflow** (4 divergent concepts — bold-type magazine / colour-coded system / asymmetric canvas / numbered hub — scored by 3 judges on distinctiveness, brand-flat-fit, *handles-image-less-elegantly*, premium feel, buildability, a11y) picked **"The Editorial" (type-as-cover magazine)** as the winner, with a decisive graft the judges agreed on: it was pure-type with no visual anchor, so graft **Concept 3's "Index Plate"** richness — per-topic **glyphs**, a bled **folio numeral**, a flat **SVG dot texture**, and a single **cyan rule** — to give each image-less card a real designed visual.

**The image-less fix — `CoverTile` (new):** a designed magazine "cover plate" built 100% from real metadata (never a fabricated photo, never a gradient). A flat field in **3 deterministic variants** (`ink` deep-navy / `paper` navy-tint / `rule` white-with-a-cyan-spine, chosen by a stable per-slug hash with an adjacent-collision tie-break so even the 3-article launch looks varied), a large low-opacity **per-topic glyph** (`salary→IndianRupee`, `early-career→GraduationCap`, `resume→FileText`, `applying→Send`, `portfolio→Briefcase`, fallback `Sparkles`) opposite a bled decorative **folio numeral** ("01/02/03", `aria-hidden`, never framed as a sequential step), a flat **SVG `<pattern>` of `<circle>` dots** (a vector shape — deliberately not a CSS gradient — id derived from the slug for uniqueness), one **cyan editorial rule**, and the **title set big as the artwork**. Renders a real `coverImageUrl` on the image path when one ever exists.

**Layout (`article-visuals.ts` + 4 section components):** wrapped in `SiteShell` (route stays dynamic). **`CareerMasthead`** — a magazine issue-line (identity + real "N stories · Updated {month year}"), an oversized wordmark ("Career advice, *worth reading*"), a dek, and the `ArticleSearch` (`?q=`). **`ContentsRail`** — the real topics as an uppercase letterspaced editorial index with counts + a navy active state (desktop lg+; the page falls back to the `TagFilter` chip row on mobile — exactly one topic nav in the a11y tree per breakpoint). Featured **cover plate** (`CoverTile size="cover" variant="ink" headingLevel={2}`, default view page-1 only). A 2-up **`CoverTile` grid** with per-tile variants. Restyled Newer/Older pagination. **`CareerColophon`** — a flat deep-navy closing band (reusing the same dot texture) pairing the real Browse-jobs/Job-alert CTA with a conditional "archive" list (recent stories not on the current screen — empty at 3 articles). Retired **`FeaturedArticle`/`CareerHero`/`CareerSidebar`** (subsumed); the shared **`ArticleCard` is untouched** (the homepage's articles section can't regress).

**Adversarial review** (Workflow, 4 lenses — correctness/RSC · a11y · brand-contrast · isolation — each finding independently refuted): **2 distinct issues, both fixed.** (MED, confirmed by 4 lenses) the featured `CoverTile` emitted its title as `<h3>` directly under the masthead `<h1>` with no intervening `<h2>` (WCAG 1.3.1 heading-order skip) → `CoverTile` gained a `headingLevel` prop and the lead story now renders `<h2>` (grid cards stay `<h3>`); browser-verified the order is now `h1→h2→h2→h3→h3→h2` with no skip. (LOW) the default **0-articles** view rendered the *filtered* "Nothing matches yet / try a different search" copy → branched the empty state on `q||tag` so the default shows "No articles yet / Check back soon"; browser-verified by unpublishing all 3 articles then restoring. A `fg-subtle`→`fg-muted` contents-rail count contrast miss was also caught + fixed during the self-audit.

**Gate (integrated, develop @ `b379ff2`):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API 629 / web **188** / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, real data): the featured ink plate renders the SVG dots + glyph + "01" numeral; the two grid tiles render **different** variants (a navy-tint `paper` + a white `rule` with its cyan spine); heading order `[1,2,2,3,3,2]` no skip; **no horizontal overflow at 1360 / 768 / 375** (the featured title `clamp()` shrinks 56px→30px on mobile); contents rail on desktop → chip row on mobile; `?q=salary`→1 and `?tag=applying`→2 both work + drive the rail active state + hide the featured; all text passes AA (ink kicker/CTA accent-400-on-navy 8.7:1, ink title 19.5:1, ink excerpt 12.7:1, paper kicker 5.35:1, contents-rail active 15.4:1, masthead 5.84:1, colophon 8.7–16:1); 0 console errors; the homepage articles section (shared `ArticleCard`) is unaffected.

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** the image-less "cover plate" is the honest answer to the missing-photos request — if real article photography is ever added (a CMS/upload effort), `CoverTile`'s image path renders it automatically. The article **reading page** (`/career-advice/[slug]`) remains out of scope (the brief targets the hub). With exactly 1 published article the grid section is suppressed (featured-only), which now reads cleanly.

### `bugfix/company-logo-image-host` — web CompanyLogo crashed next/image on API-served logos · 2026-07-20

**Bugfix** (reported by a teammate: the seeker site crashed on open). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **`apps/web` only** — one prop + a comment on `apps/web/components/companies/CompanyLogo.tsx`; **no schema/migration/flag/API/lock**; other apps/packages untouched.

**Symptom:** a `next/image` runtime error on the seeker homepage — *"Invalid src prop (http://localhost:4000/media/company-logos/7-…jpg) on `next/image`, hostname 'localhost' is not configured under images in your `next.config.js`"* — with the stack `CompanyLogo → FeaturedJobCard → FeaturedJobs`.

**Root cause:** company logos uploaded via the recruiter portal are served from the API `/media` passthrough (`StorageService.getPublicUrl` returns `${apiBase}/media/${key}`), which in dev is `http://localhost:4000/media/company-logos/…`. `next.config.ts` `images.remotePatterns` allowlists only the R2/CDN hosts (`*.r2.cloudflarestorage.com`, `cdn.jobportal.com`, `imagedelivery.net`) — **not** the API host. The web `CompanyLogo` used an **optimized** `next/image`, so Next tried to proxy-fetch the un-allowlisted host and threw. Latent for the whole project (the demo seed sets every `logoUrl` to NULL → initials fallback); a teammate uploaded a real logo for company 7, and the new homepage `FeaturedJobs` section surfaced it — but it would equally crash `/jobs`, `/companies`, and the company-detail page for that company.

**Fix:** add the **`unoptimized`** prop to the web `CompanyLogo`'s `<Image>` — small, already-sized logos served as-is, bypassing the optimizer + host allowlist. This **mirrors `apps/recruiter/components/CompanyLogo.tsx`, which already carries `unoptimized` + a comment describing this exact failure** — the web copy simply missed it. `CompanyLogo` is the only web component that renders API-served images (article covers come from R2/CDN, already allowlisted), so this one change fixes every affected surface. Chose `unoptimized` over allowlisting the API host because it's environment-agnostic (no per-env `remotePatterns` config), matches the established recruiter-app pattern, and the images are tiny.

**Gate (integrated, develop @ the teammate's `feature/recruiter-plans-always-visible` tip):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** · build **4/4**. **Browser-verified** against the exact reported scenario: temporarily set company 7 (Rasta Logistics)'s `logoUrl` to the API-media URL from the error, then loaded the **company detail page** and the **homepage `FeaturedJobs`** — the logo renders as a raw **unoptimized `<img>`** (`isNextOptimizerProxy: false`), the page content renders (`h1`/section present), **no "hostname not configured" error**, 0 console errors. **DB restored** (company 7 `logoUrl` → NULL; 0 companies with a logo, back to the seed state).

**Follow-up:** none required. If optimization of production logos ever matters, the alternative is a dynamic `remotePatterns` entry derived from the API URL — but `unoptimized` is the intended pattern here (per the recruiter app).

### `feature/recruiter-plans-always-visible` — Plans & Billing visible to every recruiter (Free = default plan) · 2026-07-20

**Recruiter Plans & Pricing made permanently visible, with the Free plan as the explicit default state** (SRS §4.11 / §7). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **Recruiter + API + packages** — `apps/web` (job-seeker) + `apps/services` **byte-untouched** (`git diff develop…HEAD -- apps/web apps/services` empty). **No schema change, no migration, no `theme.css`, no `packages/types`.**

**The core decomposition: VISIBILITY vs PURCHASABILITY.** Previously one flag — `subscription.system.enabled`, seeded OFF — did both jobs, so the entire surface was invisible and 404'd: L1 recruiter middleware, the sidebar Billing group, and both L2 pages all read it. The owner asked for the tab to be visible to all recruiters (who are all implicitly on a Free plan). Rather than flip that flag, this splits the concerns.

**Why not simply turn `subscription.system.enabled` ON** (the important finding): it is **not recruiter-scoped**. `apps/api/src/applications/quota.service.ts` reads it to set `upgradeAvailable`, which flows to `apps/web/components/job/ApplyButton.tsx` and renders *"Upgrade your plan to apply to more jobs today. **See plans →**"* linking to **`/pricing`** — a route that **does not exist in `apps/web`** and is gated by a *different* flag (`subscription.pricing_page.visible`, also OFF). Flipping the master switch would therefore have shipped a **404 link to rate-limited job seekers**, violating the "don't disturb the seeker portal" constraint. It stays OFF and now means *purchasable* only, still enforced at L3 by `assertBillingEnabled` (API untouched).

**Flag** (**`feature-flags/keys.ts` lock taken + released**): new **`recruiter.plans_visible`** (`RECRUITER_PLANS_VISIBLE`), seeded **`enabled: true` ⇒ 41 flags**. `FlagSeed` gained an optional `enabled` (defaults `false`, so no existing flag changed) — this is a *visibility* flag for a free surface, not a paid capability, so seeding it ON is consistent with CLAUDE.md §0. It gates L1 (`middleware.ts`), the nav (`layout.tsx` → `SidebarNav showBilling`) and L2 (`plans`/`billing` `notFound()`), and doubles as the killswitch to hide the surface again with no redeploy.

**`/plans` changes:** the per-tier `subscription.plans.<tier>.enabled` keys **no longer filter cards out of the catalogue** — they now compute a per-plan `purchasable` (`master && tier`) that swaps the CTA for a **"Coming soon"** status line, so pricing is always reviewable. New synthetic **`FreePlanCard`** (deliberately **no DB row** — no `tier: FREE` plan exists and a company with no `Subscription` already resolves to FREE via the tier-resolver; a real ₹0 row would ripple into the plan list, per-tier flags and purchase path for no gain) renders first, badged **"Current plan"** when `currentPlanId === null`. Its bullets are intentionally **number-free** because the post limits are env-configurable. The page opts into **`data-wide`** (`max-w-6xl`, the Jobs-list pattern) and the grid moves to `lg:grid-cols-4` so all four cards sit on one row instead of stranding Enterprise. RECRUITER plans now ship **listed** (`isActive`/`isPublic`) since *listed ≠ purchasable*; **CANDIDATE plans stay `false`/`false`** so the `apps/web` storefront remains dark.

**Adversarial review** (4 lenses — security/enforcement, react/a11y, cross-app isolation, product-copy truthfulness — each finding independently refuted): **13 raised → 8 confirmed, all fixed; 5 refuted.** (HIGH) the purchase-closed banner keyed off `!purchaseEnabled` alone, so a company holding an ACTIVE paid subscription while an admin had the master switch off was told *"your team is on the Free plan"* on the same screen its paid card showed "Current plan" → banner now consults `currentPlanId`, with a distinct "Plan changes are paused" message; (HIGH) plan descriptions sold **ResDex, bulk messaging and priority support — all three entirely unimplemented** (flag keys and seed strings only, no route/controller/service) and "**Unlimited** postings", directly contradicting the deliberately careful "Higher job-posting limits" bullet label → copy corrected, and RECRUITER `name`/`description` now re-sync on reseed (price/flags/listing state still admin-owned) so stale copy can't survive a fix; (MED) `/billing` newly exposed a billing-details form whose `PUT` is behind `assertBillingEnabled`, so a Day-0 owner could type their GSTIN and get a 403 → hidden while purchasing is closed; (MED) the seed's blanket `updateMany` forced `isActive/isPublic` on every reseed, overriding admin intent and leaving **no supported way to delist a plan** (contradicting the flags seed's own `update: {}` rule) → narrowed to rows still in the unlisted default, and `enable-recruiter-billing.ts --off` no longer de-lists; (MED) a Member saw a "Coming soon" footer on unbuyable cards but **no footer at all** on buyable ones, making the unreachable tier look more complete → added an "Owners and admins only" note; (MED) "every feature you use today stays free" was an unbounded promise the flag system can't keep → present tense; (NIT) the new flag fell through `cache-purge`'s defensive `['/']` default and would have purged the **job-seeker homepage** → mapped to `[]` **+1 API test (629)**; (LOW) stale comments the change falsified. Separately, the "Coming soon" affordance was changed from a disabled `<Button>` (**3.39:1** via the shared `disabled:opacity-50`, unfixable without editing `packages/ui` and rippling into `apps/web`) to status **text** — semantically honest since nobody can act on it, and **7.79:1**.

**Gate (integrated, develop @ 2d4f0f3):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API **629** (+1) / web 188 / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, demo recruiter Priya @ Nimbus, at the **true Day-0 flag state** — note `subscription.system.enabled` had been left **ON** in the local DB by an earlier demo and was reset to OFF first): the **Billing** group appears in the sidebar with billing OFF (previously hidden) with both sub-items; `/plans` shows Free badged "Current plan" + three priced paid cards each reading "Coming soon"; `/billing` shows the Free summary + empty payment history (both routes were **404** before); a **MEMBER sees the identical page** (role temporarily flipped to MEMBER and restored); both banner branches confirmed; **OWNER + flags ON still yields real "Choose plan" buttons**, proving the purchase path is intact; the **killswitch** (flag OFF) 404s `/plans`+`/billing` and removes the nav group while `/dashboard`+`/jobs` stay 200; a near-expiry job's `JobValidityCard` **Upgrade** CTA now reaches a live `/plans` (it was previously hidden precisely because `/plans` 404'd); 4-up / 2×2 / 1-up at 1280/768/375 with **no horizontal overflow**; dark mode all AA (10.2–17.2:1); **0 console errors**. **DB fully restored**: temporary `Subscription` deleted (0 rows), Priya back to OWNER with 7 ACTIVE jobs, job 100007's `expiresAt` restored byte-exact, all four purchase flags OFF, `recruiter.plans_visible` ON.

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** the Enterprise card still renders **"ResDex candidate database" / "Bulk candidate messaging" bullets** from its `featureKeys` — the *descriptions* were corrected, but stripping the tier's only feature list is a product/marketing call left to the owner (both features remain unbuilt). `recruiter.plans_visible` is intentionally **not** classified critical: adding it would require editing `apps/web/lib/admin/types.ts`, which hand-duplicates `CRITICAL_FLAG_KEYS` — and touching `apps/web` was out of bounds; leaving the two lists in sync was judged safer than a partial fix. Seed prices remain the documented placeholders (the banner labels them a preview). `createOrder`'s `isActive/isPublic` predicate is no longer an independent second barrier now that RECRUITER rows ship listed — the purchase gate rests on the two flag reads.

### `feature/career-advice-redesign` — Career Advice hub → premium editorial index · 2026-07-19

**Career Advice hub (`/career-advice`) redesign** (SRS §4.8). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **`apps/web` only** — `packages/*` + other apps + `theme.css`/`globals.css` byte-untouched; **no schema/migration/feature-flag/API/routing/shared-surface-lock** (career-advice barrel edit is append-only). Scope was set with the owner up front (AskUserQuestion): **omit** every no-data section, build a **focused editorial index** (not the full 15-section magazine), and use a **typographic image-ready** treatment. The decisive constraint: the real data is **3 published articles, zero with cover images, one author ("JobPortal Editorial"), ~5 tags** — no views, no saved/bookmark model, no author profiles, no category field, no featured/editor flag. So ~90% of the brief's requested sections (newsletter, popular-authors, roadmaps, learning-collections, career-guides, free-resource downloads, related-services links, most-read/trending/views, bookmark) have **no backing** and were **omitted** rather than shipped as placeholder UI.

**Design process:** a **high-fidelity mockup** (visualize) confirmed the typographic direction; the design language was otherwise tightly constrained (reuse `ArticleCard`/`TagFilter`/`Card`/`Badge`, flat brand), so no judge panel was needed.

**Layout.** Wrapped in **`SiteShell`** (shared header+footer) + `Container` — the page previously rendered a bare `<main>` with **no navbar/footer** (the same chrome gap company-detail had). This makes the route **dynamic** (SiteHeader reads auth server-side), so `revalidate` was dropped (same trade-off `/companies` + `/company` took); the BreadcrumbList JSON-LD + self-canonical still render. **`CareerHero`** — eyebrow + headline ("Career advice and professional insights") + one-line promise + the **`ArticleSearch`** client input driving **`?q=`** (title/excerpt `contains`, insensitive; prefilled from the URL; clear button; resets pagination; composes with `?tag=`). **`FeaturedArticle`** — the newest article as a visually dominant, typographic lead story (category = top tag, title stretched-link, excerpt, author monogram · date · read-time, "Continue reading"); shown ONLY in the default unfiltered page-1 view; image-ready (renders a cover alongside the copy if one exists). A **2-column body** (`lg:[minmax(0,1fr)_280px]`): **main** = "Latest articles" grid (enhanced `ArticleCard`) + Newer/Older pagination (preserves `q`+`tag`); **sticky sidebar** (lg+) = **`CareerSidebar`** (Browse-by-topic real tag list + counts with active state, Recently-published list, and one real cross-product CTA — Browse jobs / Set a job alert). The topic nav lives in the sidebar on desktop and swaps to a horizontal `TagFilter` chip row on tablet/mobile (`lg:hidden`) where the sidebar hides — exactly one topic nav in the a11y tree per breakpoint.

**Shared `ArticleCard` enhancement.** Rebuilt as a flat, full-height `<article>` with a whole-card stretched link, a category badge (top tag), an author monogram, and `fg-muted` meta (was `fg-subtle`, sub-AA). It is **also consumed by the homepage's `RecentArticles` section** — verified no regression (3 cards, stretched links, no overflow, 0 console errors on `/`).

**Adversarial review** (Workflow, 4 lenses — correctness/RSC · a11y · brand-contrast · isolation — each finding independently refuted): **1 confirmed → fixed.** (MED, flagged by 2 lenses) with exactly **one** published article, the default view hoists it into the featured slot → `gridArticles` empties → the grid's "No articles yet" empty-state fired **below the visible featured card**, contradicting the "1 article" count. Gated the empty state on the true absence of content (`gridArticles.length === 0 && !featured`); **browser-verified by temporarily unpublishing 2 of the 3 articles** (featured + "1 article" render, no empty box) then **restoring all 3 to PUBLISHED**. A `fg-subtle`→`fg-muted` topic-count contrast miss was also caught + fixed during the self-audit.

**Gate (integrated, develop @ 09e342f — develop hadn't moved):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API 628 / web **188** (+2: the new `?q=` parser tests — the exact-shape default test + trim/blank/array/80-cap coverage; the gate caught the shape change and I updated it) / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, anon, real data): `?q=salary` → 1 result (no featured, prefilled input, "Results for…" heading); `?tag=applying` → 2 results + sidebar "Applying" active; search+tag compose; layout measured `824/280` at 1360px, sidebar folds + mobile topic chips appear at 768, single column at 375; **no horizontal overflow at 1360/768/375**; all text AA (hero eyebrow 5.58:1, featured label 5.84:1, category badges 15.2:1, meta 7.79:1, sidebar active 15.2:1, CTA 15.4:1); 0 console errors; homepage articles section unaffected.

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** the full magazine (newsletter, popular-authors, roadmaps, learning-collections, career-guides, free-resources, related-services, most-read/trending, bookmark) is omitted for lack of any backing model/data — each needs its own model/API before it can ship non-fabricated. The article **reading page** (`/career-advice/[slug]`) was out of scope (the brief's structure is the hub). With exactly 1 published article the "Latest articles" grid renders empty beneath the featured card (harmless empty `<ul>`, no longer contradictory) — a rare transient state not worth special-casing further.

### `feature/navbar-hire-cta-restyle` — navbar employer CTA restyle + rename · 2026-07-19

**Navbar "Hire" CTA restyle + rephrase** (SRS §4.1). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **`apps/web` only** — shared `HeaderAuthActions.tsx` (desktop navbar) + `MobileMenu.tsx` (drawer); `theme.css`/other apps/packages byte-untouched; **no schema/migration/flag/API/routing/lock**. The `SiteHeader` "Hire on Career Queue →" button was **too wide** for the nav bar. Two changes: (1) **rephrased** to **"Hire talent"** — same meaning (recruit here), ~half the width (250px → 123px measured); (2) **restyled** from a solid cyan fill to a **cyan outline that fills on hover** — rest: white bg + cyan border + cyan label; hover: solid cyan fill + white text; **150ms (`--duration-fast`) ease-out** colour transition on bg/border/text together.

**Colour (owner-directed):** all three cyan surfaces use the **EXACT brand cyan `--color-accent-500` (#22A0DA — the logo-arrow cyan)**, per an explicit owner correction after an initial pass used the darker AA-safe `accent-700`. This is a deliberate **brand-fidelity-over-AA** choice: the exact cyan lands at **~2.95:1** in every role (cyan text on white, cyan border on white, white text on the cyan hover fill) — below WCAG AA (4.5:1 for text / 3:1 for the UI boundary). Recorded transparently; the AA-safe path (accent-600 border + accent-700 text/fill, ~4–5.85:1) or navy-on-cyan fill text (7.11:1) remain available if the owner later wants readability restored. The mobile-drawer CTA was kept consistent (filled accent-500 + white) and rephrased to match.

**Gate:** `pnpm --filter web typecheck` ✓ + `pnpm build` **4/4** ✓. The full unit-test suite (API 628 / web 186 / …) was **not re-run** — the change is className + button-label text only, with zero unit-test surface (no test asserts on these classes or copy), consistent with treating a cosmetic CSS/text tweak proportionally. **Browser-verified** (anon, real browser): the button computes to `#23a0da` (= the `accent-500` token, matching #22A0DA within oklch→sRGB rounding), width 123px, rest = white bg + cyan border + cyan label, transition 0.15s `cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1)`, hover classes present (fill accent-500 + white text), 0 console errors. (The live hover animation itself isn't frame-verifiable in the hidden preview tab — transitions freeze there — but the end-state colours + transition timing are confirmed.)

**Deferred / follow-ups:** the exact-brand-cyan button is intentionally below AA (owner call) — revisit if accessibility compliance is later prioritised over exact brand fidelity. The footer's "Hire on Career Queue" text link was left as-is (descriptive footer label, not width-constrained).

### `feature/home-redesign` — search-first homepage redesign + flat brand + Latest Jobs · 2026-07-19

**Public homepage redesign** (SRS §4.1). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **`apps/web` only** — the shared **navbar (`SiteHeader`/`ScrollHeaderChrome`) + footer (`SiteFooter`) are byte-untouched** (owner decision), and **`packages/ui/src/styles/theme.css` + `apps/web/app/globals.css` were NOT modified** (so the `.glass`/`.gradient-*` utilities + `--gradient-*`/`--glass-*` tokens stay defined for their other consumers — `SrpSearchExpand`, `AuthModal`, `OnboardingWizard`, `CitySelect`, the footer — and the §15-locked theme surface is never touched). `apps/services` + `apps/recruiter` + `apps/api` + `packages/*` byte-untouched. **No schema/migration/feature-flag/API/routing/shared-surface-lock.** Scope was set with the owner up front (AskUserQuestion): **refresh** the hero, **keep** the navbar + footer, **omit** testimonials/newsletter/app (no backing data), **reorder** the IA search-first, and **flatten** the sections.

**Design process:** a **judge-panel Workflow** (3 diverse concepts — Naukri-density / Linear-restraint / LinkedIn-balance — scored by 3 judges on search-first IA, brand-flat fit, premium feel, data-honesty, a11y, buildability) returned a **unanimous winner, "The Clean Index"** (51/52/52). Its per-file flattening plan + two safety grafts (do-not-delete the shared tokens; reuse the already-shared paise salary formatter) were adopted.

**Flattening (the core brand fix):** the homepage's content sections still carried gradients (`--gradient-brand`, `--gradient-brand-soft`, `gradient-border`) + glassmorphism (`.glass`/`.glass-lg`) + `card-lift` hover-lift — all banned by the owner's flat-brand direction (and on the brief's own avoid-list). Every content section was flattened: `glass` cards → flat `border + bg-elevated` surfaces; `bg-[image:var(--gradient-brand-soft)]` icon chips → flat `bg-primary-100`; `--gradient-brand` spotlight/eyebrow ticks → flat `bg-accent-500`; radial cyan section washes + blurred cyan blobs removed; `card-lift` → flat `transition-colors` border-hover (matching the SRP card + company-detail cards); `RecruiterCta`'s full navy→cyan gradient panel → a **flat deep-navy #192249 finale**. Browser-verified **0 `backdrop-filter`** across the content and only the hero's neutral single-colour dot texture remaining.

**New search-first IA:** Hero (search console + a live **count ribbon**) → **Latest jobs (8 real `FeaturedJobCard`s)** → Companies hiring now → Discover jobs by role → Jobs by city → Explore by industry → In-demand skills → Why JobPortal (BentoValue) → From the team (Articles) → FAQ → flat-navy Recruiter finale. Roles/Cities kept as clean stacked full-width sections rather than refactoring two components into a 2-col split (avoids duplicating their card markup).

**Featured/Latest Jobs (the real IA gap):** the homepage showed no dedicated jobs section — only 3 jobs inside a now-dead hero cluster. Repurposed the formerly-dead `heroJobs` query (`take 3 → 8`; type `HeroJob` → `FeaturedJob`; field `heroJobs` → `latestJobs`), added a new flat **`FeaturedJobCard`** (logo · 2-line title stretched-link · company · city · salary · work-mode badge · posted age + a cyan "New" dot for ≤1-day posts) that reuses the **already-shared paise `formatSalaryLpa` / `postedAgo` / `WORK_MODE_LABELS`** from `lib/job/format.ts` (no new formatter, no invented skills/experience — only fields the query carries), and a `FeaturedJobs` section. **Deleted** the dead glass `HeroJobCluster.tsx`.

**Hero refresh + count ribbon:** search-first headline **"Find your next job in India."** (word "job" in `accent-600`), sub reworked, the `HeroSearchBar` flattened (`glass-lg` → flat `border + bg-elevated + shadow-card`), quick-filter chips flattened off the `white/60`/`white/80` translucency. The standalone `TrustStrip` stats band was **deleted** and folded into a slim live **count ribbon** under the search (activeJobs / companies / hiring teams via `CountUp` + "Free for job seekers" — preserving TrustStrip's freemium ₹0 message). A handful of `fg-subtle` labels (skill-count, company rating/openings, bento proof) were bumped to `fg-muted` for AA.

**Adversarial review** (Workflow, 4 lenses — correctness/RSC · a11y · brand-contrast · isolation — each finding independently refuted): **1 confirmed → fixed** (NIT: a stale "glass chips" header comment in `PopularSkillsGrid` after the chips were flattened). All three substantive lenses returned clean / all-refuted — notably confirming the RSC server/client boundaries, the count-ribbon markup validity, and that the shared-token safety held.

**Gate (integrated, develop @ b9486ad — develop hadn't moved):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API 628 / web **186** / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19 — unchanged; these home components have no unit-test harness, consistent with prior seeker UI PRs) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, anon visitor, real data): search-first section order exact; 8 real jobs at **4 / 2 / 1** columns (desktop/tablet/mobile); the live count ribbon; **0 `backdrop-filter`** (glassmorphism fully removed) with only the neutral dot texture as a "gradient"; **no horizontal overflow at 1360 / 768 / 375**; all text passes AA (headline "job" 3.82:1 at 48px/700 large-text; ribbon accent-700 5.58:1; featured salary 17.9:1; skill-count 7.14:1; recruiter eyebrow accent-400-on-navy 6.89:1; New badge accent-700-on-accent-50 5.46:1); **0 console errors**; and — because `HeroSearchBar` is **shared with `SrpSearchExpand`** — verified the flattened bar still renders correctly when the SRP search pops out (flat white surface, real border, **no backdrop-filter**, 3 fields) → no cross-surface regression.

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** testimonials/newsletter/mobile-app omitted for lack of any backing model/endpoint (would have been fabricated UI). The panel's 2-column "by role | by city" density pairing was not taken (kept stacked full-width to avoid a two-component refactor + card-markup duplication). The dead `HowItWorks`/`WhyJobPortal` home components remain exported-but-unused (left as-is — out of scope). The navbar's own scroll-chrome (`ScrollHeaderChrome`) + the footer still use gradients — intentionally out of scope (owner kept nav + footer).

### `feature/company-detail-redesign` — seeker Company Detail page redesign · 2026-07-19

**Company Detail page redesign** (SRS §4.7). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **`apps/web` only** — `apps/services` + `apps/recruiter` + `apps/api` + `packages/*` **byte-untouched** (`git diff --name-only develop…HEAD` is entirely under `apps/web/`); **no schema/migration/feature-flag/shared-surface-lock** (companies barrel edit is append-only). Transforms the public `/company/[handle]` profile from a single-column bare-`<main>` stub — which rendered under the root layout with **no navbar/footer at all** (a real chrome-consistency gap: `/companies` and `/job/[slug]` had both already adopted `SiteShell`) — into a premium, enterprise-grade layout comparable to LinkedIn/Glassdoor company pages with Linear/Stripe restraint.

**Layout.** Wrapped in **`SiteShell`** (shared header + footer) + `Container size="lg"` + a `Breadcrumbs` row. A content-first **hero card**: logo (88px) · name (h1) · **dormant-until-KYC `VerifiedBadge`** · industry + company type · `RatingStars` · icon-led fact row (HQ / employees / founded / website) · primary actions (**Share** + **View jobs**) · a real-metrics **stat strip** (Open roles · Rating · Reviews · Hiring status). Below the hero, a responsive **3-column grid** `lg:[220px_1fr] xl:[220px_1fr_300px]`: **left** = sticky scroll-spy in-page nav (`CompanyProfileNav`) + `CompanyQuickFacts`; **main** = About → Highlights → Open positions → Reviews; **right** (xl, sticky) = `CompanyHiringRail` (active-count + View-all + Job-alert CTA) + `RelatedCompanies`. Below each breakpoint where a sidebar hides, its **pure** content folds inline into the main column (Quick Facts `<lg`, Hiring + Related `<xl`) — the related-companies query resolves **once** in the page and is shared across breakpoints (no double query, no duplicate ids since the folded copies carry none).

**Data honesty.** Built strictly around the real `Company` model. Reliably present (all 12 demo companies): name, description, industry, HQ, employee band, founded, rating + review count, active jobs, reviews. Built-but-**dormant** (null in seed, light up when a recruiter fills them): `logoUrl` (→ initials monogram), `companyType`, `workingAtSections` (→ `CompanyHighlights`, parsed defensively), and **KYC** (`VerifiedBadge` shows only on `status==='VERIFIED'`). **Not fabricated** (no backing model): a follow system / followers count, galleries, funding/awards timelines, recruiter-contact + response-time metrics, salary/resume widgets — deliberately omitted rather than shipped as fake UI. **Share** is the only share affordance (Web Share API + copy-link fallback, no backend).

**Components** (`apps/web/components/companies/`): **new** — `CompanyProfileHero`, `CompanyStatStrip`, `CompanyShareButton` (client), `CompanyProfileNav` (client, IntersectionObserver scroll-spy), `CompanyQuickFacts`, `CompanyHighlights`, `CompanyHiringRail`, `RelatedCompanies` (pure; same-industry peers via one `findMany` + one bounded `job.groupBy` for live-role counts), `VerifiedBadge`, `company-format.ts` (companyType labels + host formatter). **Enhanced in place** (logic unchanged, + card wrapper + section `id`/`scroll-mt`): `CompanyAbout`, `CompanyOpenings`, `CompanyReviews` (now takes rating aggregates for a summary header). The old **`CompanyHero` is left intact** — it is still used by `app/[...path]/_handlers/working-at.tsx` (regression-checked: both `working-at` routes still 200).

**Rendering.** `SiteShell` resolves signed-in state server-side (cookies), so the route is now **dynamic** and `export const revalidate` was dropped — the same trade-off `/companies` + `/job/[slug]` already took. Structured data (Organization + BreadcrumbList JSON-LD), the self-canonical, and the slug-drift **308 permanentRedirect** are all preserved.
