The Prisma `select` was widened with `companyType` / `workingAtSections` / `industryId` / `kyc.status`.

**Adversarial review** (Workflow, 4 lenses — correctness/RSC · a11y · brand-contrast · isolation — each finding independently refuted): **1 confirmed → fixed.** (LOW a11y) invalid `<dl>`: a `<div>` grouping child of `<dl>` may contain only `<dt>`/`<dd>`, so the "Not yet rated" `<p>` sub-text sibling was disallowed → nested inside the `<dd>` as a block `<span>` (browser-verified: no invalid `<p>` under the grouping). The other three lenses returned clean / all-refuted. Two further contrast issues were caught + fixed during the self-audit before the review: the green "Active" stat was 2.77:1 (sub-AA) → high-contrast `--color-fg` text + a decorative colored dot (16.4:1); and `fg-subtle` (~2.6:1, decorative-only) was misused for meaningful text in a few labels/meta → `fg-muted` (7.79:1).

**Gate (integrated, develop @ b71299d — develop hadn't moved):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API **628** / web **186** / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19 — unchanged; these UI components have no unit-test harness, consistent with prior seeker UI PRs) · build **4/4** apps (`/company/[handle]` now builds as **`ƒ` dynamic**). **Browser-verified** (real browser, Nimbus + Sahaj Pay): 3-column layout measured `220/568/300` at 1360px with exactly one visible instance of each folded section; **zero horizontal overflow at 1360 / 768 / 375**; stat strip 4-across desktop → 2-across mobile; hero stacks on mobile; sticky rails at `top-20`; dormant paths verified by temporarily setting a VERIFIED KYC + `companyType` (badge + type + quick-facts row rendered) then **fully restoring the DB**; related companies renders for a company with peers (Nimbus → Veridian + Sutra Labs) and is correctly absent for the sole Banking company (Sahaj Pay); all text passes AA (Active 16.4:1, labels/meta 7.79:1, website link `accent-700` 5.84:1, verified badge 15.2:1); 0 console errors; header/footer chrome present. Every DB fixture tweaked during verification was **restored** (Nimbus KYC removed + type nulled; Sutra Labs rating back to 4.5/2).

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** a real **Follow / Save-company** feature (with a `CandidateFollowedCompany` model + API + a followers count) is a separate task — omitted here rather than shipped as non-functional UI. The **scroll-spy active-highlight** could not be frame-verified in the hidden preview tab (IntersectionObserver is paused when `document.hidden`) — the anchor scroll + `scroll-mt` offset works and the logic is standard IO that runs in a real visible tab. Rich Highlights (`workingAtSections`) + `companyType` + the Verified badge stay dormant until the recruiter-side write paths / KYC populate them.

### `feature/recruiter-job-collaborate` — Job Detail Collaborate (per-job collaborators) · 2026-07-18

**Recruiter Job Detail "Collaborate" — PR B of two** (SRS §4.9). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **Recruiter + API + packages** — `apps/web` (job-seeker) + `apps/services` **byte-untouched** (`git diff develop…HEAD -- apps/web apps/services` empty). Completes the job-detail enhancement started in `feature/recruiter-job-detail-actions` (PR A, the display/actions half). Lets a job **owner** grant company teammates access so they can **manage or respond to** the job — the deliberate deferral the original Job Detail page recorded ("company-wide teammate access = a broader RBAC decision") is now taken, as an **additive per-job allowlist** rather than a blanket company-wide broadening.

**Access model** (owner-approved): a **collaborator** can view the job detail, **edit** it, **close/reopen** it, and **act on its applicants** (transition status / notes / resume — "respond to this job"). The **owner** additionally can **delete**, **publish** a draft, and **add/remove collaborators**; those stay owner-strict. Only the owner manages collaborators (v1).

**Schema** (migration **`add_job_collaborators`**; **DB-schema lock taken + released**): new **`model JobCollaborator`** — `id`, `jobId`→`Job`(Cascade), `userId`→`User`(Cascade, relation `"CollaboratingJobs"`), `addedById Int?` (acting owner, loose actor), `createdAt`, `@@unique([jobId,userId])` + `userId`/`jobId` indexes. **Keyed by User id** (not Recruiter id) so the owner-or-collaborator check is a plain OR against `postedById`/JWT `sub`. Back-relations `Job.collaborators` + `User.collaboratingJobs`. Enum **`NotificationType += JOB_COLLABORATION`**. (The migration also folds in a pre-existing `JobAlert.unsubscribeToken` default reconciliation Prisma detected — benign, no behaviour change.)

**Feature flag** (**`feature-flags/keys.ts` lock taken + released**): new **`killswitch.recruiter_job_collaborate`** (`KILL_RECRUITER_JOB_COLLABORATE`) seeded **OFF ⇒ collaboration LIVE** ⇒ **40 flags**. Auto-classified critical by the `killswitch.*` prefix rule (Slack + confirm-modal, no `isCriticalFlag` edit). Enforcement: **L3** — the add/remove endpoints 503 when ON; **L2** — the Job Detail page hides the Collaborate control. Existing collaborators keep their access (the switch gates *granting* new access, matching the job-delete killswitch scope).

**API** (`apps/api`): new **`recruiter-job-collaborators`** module — **`GET /recruiter/jobs/:id/collaborators`** (owner-only; returns current collaborators + assignable = active company recruiters minus owner minus existing), **`POST`** `{ userId }` (owner-only + killswitch; validates the target is an **active recruiter in the same company**, is **idempotent**, and fires `NotificationsProducerService.notifyJobCollaboration` fire-and-log), **`DELETE /:userId`** (owner-only + killswitch; idempotent). Shares the `recruiter/jobs` prefix with `RecruiterJobsController` — `:id/collaborators` is a distinct path from `:id`, no route conflict (verified in the boot log). A new shared **`jobManageableWhere(userId)`** (`recruiter-jobs/job-access.ts`) returns the `OR: [{ postedById }, { collaborators: { some: { userId } } }]` clause and broadens the guards: `RecruiterJobsService.getOne` becomes owner-or-collaborator (backing the GET-:id endpoint + `update`/`close`/`reopen`), while **`delete` + `publish` switch to a new owner-strict `getOwnedJob`**; `RecruiterApplicantsService.list` + `manageableApplicationOrThrow` (renamed from `ownedApplicationOrThrow`, feeding `transition`/`setNotes`/`getResumeUrl`) broaden the same way. **+13 API tests** (12 in the new collaborators suite: owner-only 404, killswitch 503, self-add/cross-company/deactivated 400s, happy-path row+notification, idempotency, remove; +1 applicants "a collaborator can transition"). The existing recruiter-jobs/applicants suites were updated for the `findUnique`→`findFirst` split.

**Recruiter UI** (`apps/recruiter`): `PostedByCard` gains a **Collaborators** subsection — an avatar row of current collaborators (visible to everyone with access) + an owner-only **Collaborate/Manage** button opening the new **`CollaborateDialog`** (client): lists current collaborators (Remove) and assignable teammates (Add), drives the API, and `router.refresh()`es so the card updates. New **`PersonAvatar`** (photo-or-initials, `unoptimized` image like `CompanyLogo`) shared by the card + dialog. The **detail / applicants / edit RSC pages** broaden their `postedById === session.sub` guards to owner-OR-collaborator (reads/writes split — the page guards in the RSC, the API re-checks on mutation). The Collaborate control is gated on `isOwner && !killswitch` (L2).

**Gate (integrated, develop @ 48a109d):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API **628** (+13) / web 186 / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser; demo recruiter Priya @ Nimbus, with **Rohan Mehta** temporarily moved into Nimbus to have a same-company teammate): the Collaborate dialog's assignable list showed Rohan; **Add** created the `JobCollaborator` row (`jobId 100001 / userId 3 / addedById 1`) + a **JOB_COLLABORATION notification** ("Priya Sharma added you as a collaborator on…", linkUrl `/jobs/100001`) and moved him to Collaborators; **logged in as Rohan** (collaborator, non-owner) he could **open `/jobs/100001` AND `/jobs/100001/applicants`** — both of which 404 for a non-owner without this feature — saw **no** owner-only Collaborate button, and **404'd on `/jobs/100002`** (a job he neither owns nor collaborates on); **Remove** deleted the row; the **L2 killswitch** (flag ON) hid the Collaborate control. **DB fully restored**: Rohan → company 2 (Veridian), 0 `JobCollaborator` rows, 0 `JOB_COLLABORATION` notifications, flag OFF, Priya's 7 jobs ACTIVE. (The recruiter dev server needed a `.next` clean + restart mid-verification to pick up the regenerated Prisma client — the transient "Unknown field collaborators" / "Can't resolve ./CollaborateDialog" console errors were from that pre-restart window and are stale; the fresh `pnpm build` passes.)

**On pull, teammates must run `pnpm db:generate && pnpm db:migrate:dev`** to apply `add_job_collaborators` before starting servers.

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** collaborated jobs don't appear in the collaborator's **Jobs list** (`/jobs` stays own/company-scoped) — a collaborator reaches the job via the notification/detail link; a "Shared with me" list is a follow-up. Only the **owner** manages collaborators (company Owners/Admins managing any company job's collaborators via `companyRole` was scoped out). The killswitch gates *granting* access, not existing collaborators (matching the job-delete switch). Collaborators get the same manage/respond rights (no per-collaborator READ_ONLY vs EDIT tier — the `Recruiter.permissions` module map stays display-only per its prior deferral).
