### `feature/recruiter-job-detail` — recruiter Job Detail page · 2026-07-18

**Recruiter Job Detail page** (SRS §4.9). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **Recruiter-only** — `apps/web` (job-seeker) + `apps/services` **byte-untouched** (`git diff develop…HEAD -- apps/web apps/services packages` empty); **no schema/migration/new feature-flag/new API endpoint/shared-surface-lock**. Reads Postgres directly in the RSC (reads/writes split). Closes the gap that clicking a published job on the recruiter Jobs list had no detail view — the title linked straight to the applicants list.

**Route:** new **`apps/recruiter/app/(authed)/jobs/[id]/page.tsx`** — owner-scoped (`int4-guarded id` + `postedById !== session.sub → notFound()`, mirroring the sibling applicants/edit pages), `force-dynamic`. Loads the job (+ company/city/locality/functionalArea), resolves `skillIds` via `skill.findMany` in declared order, and computes the five application stats with **one `application.groupBy(['status'])` + one skill-overlap `count`**. The `JobsTable` own-row title link was repointed `/jobs/[id]/applicants` → **`/jobs/[id]`** (teammate rows stay unlinked; metric-column links still go straight to the filtered applicants list).

**Six sections** (`components/jobs/detail/`): **JobDetailHeader** (title/company/location/job type/openings/posted date/status badge + View-applicants/Edit/View-public actions — all critical facts above the fold; work mode is folded into the location string via `formatJobLocation`, not a separate chip); **JobDescriptionSection** (renders the always-present plain-text `description` as paragraphs — the rich `descriptionMarkdown` render + its 8 deps live only in `apps/web`, and promoting the pipeline to a shared package would have rewired `apps/web` imports, so plain-text was chosen to keep the seeker portal untouched; **follow-up:** promote the markdown pipeline to `packages/*` for a rich render); **CandidateRequirementsCard** (experience/job type/internship duration/department/education + key-skill badges); **SalaryCompensationCard** (₹min–max LPA via `formatSalaryLpa`, or **"As per industry standards"** when both paise bounds are null — the `Job` model has **no** confidential flag, so both-null == undisclosed); **ApplicationStatsPanel** (Responses/New(`APPLIED`)/Shortlisted(`SHORTLISTED`)/Rejected(`REJECTED`)/Matches(skill overlap), each deep-linking into the applicants list filtered to that subset; a zero renders as non-link plain text, matching the Jobs-list pattern); **JobValidityCard** (date posted / valid-until / days-remaining, with **status-aware** copy + Extend→`/jobs/[id]/edit` and Upgrade→`/plans` CTAs when nearing expiry; Upgrade shown only when `subscription.system.enabled` is ON). Layout: full-width header + a `data-wide` (`max-w-6xl`) two-column grid (main + sticky sidebar) collapsing to one column on mobile.

**Supporting changes** (all `apps/recruiter`): a **`rejected`** applicant filter added to `applicant-filter.ts` (+ the applicants page where-branch/labels; `ApplicantsFilterTabs` renders it automatically) so the Rejected stat is clickable; and `job-list-format.ts` gained `formatSalaryLpa`/`formatExperienceYears`/`EMPLOYMENT_LABELS`/`WORK_MODE_LABELS`/`daysUntilExpiry` (pure functions mirrored from `apps/web/lib/job/format.ts` — that module is app-local and can't be imported across apps).

**Adversarial review** (3 lenses — correctness/security, frontend/react/a11y, isolation/regression — each finding independently verified by a refuter): **9 raised → 8 confirmed (1 refuted: a JD `break-words` nit — the render matches the seeker's `whitespace-pre-line` exactly and the app shell contains any overflow), all 8 fixed.** (MED) `JobValidityCard` was status-agnostic → a CLOSED job (reachable via the title link) showed "Expires in N days" + an Extend CTA contradicting its "Closed" badge, and a DRAFT showed "stays live until you close it" → branched the copy/CTA on status (CLOSED = closed message + no CTA; DRAFT/PENDING = not-published message + no CTA; only ACTIVE/EXPIRED get the countdown + CTAs); (MED) `daysUntilExpiry` used `Math.ceil` → a `-0` for a just-expired ACTIVE job left it flagged "Expires today" instead of expired, the "Expires today" branch was dead for future same-day expiries, and counts overstated by ~1 day → `Math.floor` (matches the codebase `postedAgo` convention); (MED) the expired-danger body text used raw `--color-danger` (~4.4:1, sub-AA) → theme-aware `color-mix(in oklch, var(--color-danger), var(--color-fg) 30%)` darkening on light / lightening on dark (the `JobStatusBadge` precedent, no new token) → **7.0:1 light / 6.7:1 dark**; (LOW) the sticky sidebar `lg:top-6` tucked under the layout's sticky `h-14` header → `lg:top-20`; (NIT) the external "View public page" link gave no new-tab AT signal → sr-only "(opens in a new tab)"; (NIT) the sibling applicants page id-guard still used `Number.isFinite` (500 vs 404 on a float/over-range id) → aligned to `isInteger` + int4-range (pre-existing, fixed while editing the file).

**Gate (integrated, develop @ 8d0f95d):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API **615** / web 186 / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19 — unchanged; the recruiter app has no unit-test harness, consistent with every prior recruiter PR) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, demo recruiter Priya @ Nimbus): all six sections render; the five stats cross-checked against Postgres for job 100001 — **exact** (Total 16 = APPLIED 9 + IN_REVIEW 4 + INTERVIEWED 2 + HIRED 1; New 9 / Shortlisted 0 / Rejected 0 / Matches 5); salary exact (380000000 paise → ₹38 LPA, 620000000 → ₹62 LPA); confidential salary → "As per industry standards"; openings chip when set; near-expiry → Extend + Upgrade, billing OFF → Upgrade hidden + Extend shown (L2 gate); EXPIRED/CLOSED/DRAFT copy + CTA all correct post-fix; the `Math.floor` just-expired case flags expired; danger contrast 7.0/6.7:1; the Rejected filter tab + deep-link + empty state; no console errors; no horizontal overflow at 1280/375; sticky 2:1 grid → single column on mobile; dark mode adapts (token-driven). Every DB fixture tweaked during verification was **restored — 100007 back to its exact original values, all 7 Priya jobs ACTIVE, the billing flag left ON (its pre-existing local state)**.

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** the Job description renders as plain text — a **rich Markdown render** would require promoting `apps/web/lib/cms/markdown.ts` (+ 8 deps) to a shared package and rewiring `apps/web`'s imports, which was out of scope under "don't disturb the seeker portal" (offered, not taken). "Extend posting" deep-links to the edit wizard (where `expiresAt` is editable) rather than a one-click **`POST /recruiter/jobs/:id/extend`** endpoint (net-new API + killswitch — deferred). The detail page is owner-only (poster-scoped `postedById`), matching the applicants/edit pages — a company-wide read-only detail for teammates would need a broader RBAC decision (kept out of scope).

### `feature/recruiter-create-publish-fields` — API: create(PUBLISH) requires city/department/openings · 2026-07-17

**API hardening — `create(PUBLISH)` now enforces the full mandatory field set** (SRS §4.9). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **API-only** — `apps/web` + `apps/services` **byte-untouched**; **no schema/migration/feature-flag/shared-surface-lock**. Closes the trust-boundary gap the prior `feature/recruiter-publish-draft` adversarial review surfaced: `CreateRecruiterJobDto` marked `primaryCityId`/`functionalAreaId`/`openings` `.optional()` with no publish-mode check and `create()` added no service-level guard, so a **direct** `POST /recruiter/jobs` with `{ publishMode:'PUBLISH', title, description }` would create a **status=ACTIVE, ES-indexed job with NULL city/department/openings**. The wizard's Publish button gates these client-side, so it's not reachable through the shipping UI — but per CLAUDE.md §4 the API is the only trusted enforcement point, and the sibling `publish()` (DRAFT→ACTIVE) already validated exactly these fields, making `create()` the inconsistent path.

**Change:** a new **`missingPublishFields(f)`** pure helper in `dto.ts` returns the human-labelled list of the mandatory-for-live fields missing/invalid on a record (title ≥3 / description ≥10 / department / openings ≥1 / city) — the **single source of truth** now used by BOTH `CreateRecruiterJobDto`'s new `publishMode==='PUBLISH'` `.refine()` (400 with a **Zod 4 dynamic `error` callback** naming only the actual gaps) AND `RecruiterJobsService.publish()` (its inline copy replaced; the thrown message is byte-identical to before), so the two make-live paths can never drift. **DRAFT saves stay lenient** — only title + description, which `baseFields` already requires. +9 `dto.test.ts` cases (16 total): DRAFT-lenient passes; PUBLISH with all fields passes; PUBLISH missing each of city/department/openings is rejected; the error names only the gaps (dept+city, not the present openings); + the helper's complete/empty/boundary (`openings:0`, short title/desc) cases.

**Adversarial review** (1 focused agent, verified empirically against the installed Zod 4.4.3 + ran the suite): **no defects.** Confirmed: `iss.input` reliably holds the parsed object when the refine fires and the callback never throws on non-object/partial input (the object parse fails first → `safeParse` returns `success:false`, no 500); no bypass (every PUBLISH missing a field → `success:false`) and no false positive (full PUBLISH → `success:true`; `openings:0`/`functionalAreaId:0` rejected by `baseFields` `min(1)`/`positive()`); composes correctly with `.strict()` + the two prior salary/experience refines (a payload with both an inverted salary range and a missing city surfaces both messages); DRAFT stays lenient. Non-defect note: `openings:0` yields two messages (base `>=1` + the refine's) — redundant but harmless/clearer.

**Gate (integrated, develop @ e0ddc4b):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API **615** (+9) / web 186 / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, demo recruiter Priya @ Nimbus): the wizard's normal **Publish** flow still works end-to-end (all fields sent → job **100238 ACTIVE**, no form error, redirect to `/jobs`) — the tightened DTO doesn't regress the UI path; the fixture was **deleted, DB restored to exactly 7 jobs**; 0 console errors. (The direct-API title-only PUBLISH → 400 path is covered by the 9 DTO unit tests running against the same schema; a mutating POST from the browser is blocked by the sandbox, so it wasn't re-exercised live.)

**Deferred / follow-ups:** none — this closes the `feature/recruiter-publish-draft` follow-up. (`openings:0` double-message is cosmetic and left as-is.)

### `feature/recruiter-publish-draft` — recruiter Publish-from-draft (DRAFT→ACTIVE) · 2026-07-17

**Recruiter "Publish a saved draft" flow** (SRS §4.9). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **Recruiter-only** — `apps/web` + `apps/services` **byte-untouched** (`git diff develop…HEAD -- apps/web apps/services` empty); **no schema/migration/new feature-flag/shared-surface-lock**. Closes the gap the prior `feature/recruiter-draft-jobs` "Draft Jobs" list exposed: a saved DRAFT could be edited/duplicated/deleted but had **no path to go live** (edit is a PATCH that never changes status; close/reopen reject DRAFT; create(PUBLISH) makes a *new* job).

**API:** new **`POST /recruiter/jobs/:id/publish`** (bodyless — publishes the STORED draft as-is). `publish()` mirrors create()'s make-live gating: `assertPostingEnabled()` (reuses **`killswitch.recruiter_post_job`** — no new flag) → `resolveRecruiterContext()` (verified-work-email hard gate, SRS §4.9.5) → ownership 404 → reject non-DRAFT (400) → **validate the stored draft's mandatory fields (title ≥3 / description ≥10 / department / openings ≥1 / city) and 400 with the exact gaps BEFORE `quota.consume`** so an incomplete draft never burns a slot → moderation flag decides ACTIVE vs PENDING_MODERATION → consume quota → flip → side-effects (ES index + alerts + cache purge) + job-posted email (ACTIVE only). The **DRAFT→final flip is an atomic `updateMany({ where: { id, status: 'DRAFT' }, … })`** (the `delete()` pattern): only the request that actually wins the transition (count 1) keeps its slot + fires side-effects; a concurrent double-click/retry/second-tab sees count 0 and **refunds** its slot — no double-consume, no duplicate "your job is live" email. The catch path also refunds on a post-consume DB failure. `postedAt` is refreshed to the go-live moment (a stale draft shouldn't rank/date as old), and a **stale past `expiresAt` is cleared** so a freshly-live job isn't expired by the very next nightly sweep.

**Recruiter UI:** the Jobs list ⋮ menu gains a **Publish** item — own **DRAFT** rows only, rendered first (the primary draft action), hidden when the post-job killswitch is ON (L2, threaded as `publishEnabled` through `page.tsx`→`JobsTable`→`JobRowMenu` like `deleteEnabled`). It opens a confirm dialog via a new `JobStatusDialog` **`publish`** action (the dialog's generic `POST /recruiter/jobs/:id/<action>` already fits; the API's 400/429/503 message surfaces inline). `packages/ui/src/icons.ts` +`Send` (append-only barrel).

**Adversarial review** (3 lenses — correctness/security, frontend/react/a11y, regression/isolation — each finding independently verified by a refuter): **11 raised → 8 confirmed (3 refuted: an unreachable verification-gate bypass ×2 and a duplicate expiry finding), all 8 fixed.** (LOW) non-atomic status guard → double-click could double-consume + double-email → atomic `updateMany` + refund-the-loser; (LOW×2) no refund on the post-consume failure/lost-race paths → `quota.refund()`; (LOW) dialog copy promised "goes live immediately / appears in search" which is false under moderation → reworded flag-agnostic ("Once it goes live…"); (LOW) stale `expiresAt` not cleared → cleared if past; (LOW) comment falsely claimed parity with create(PUBLISH) → corrected (publish() is intentionally stricter); (LOW) update-throws path untested + (NIT) missing-fields test asserted only the type → added the refund/race/verified-gate/expiry tests + a message-content assertion. Also added the verified-email gate as defense-in-depth even though the two refuters proved it currently unreachable-to-bypass (drafts can only be created by a verified recruiter and verification is monotonic) — enforcing it at the make-live boundary is non-bypassable per CLAUDE.md §4.

**Gate (integrated, develop @ e73a405):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API **606** (+10) / web 186 / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19 — the recruiter app has no unit-test harness, consistent with every prior recruiter PR) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, demo recruiter Priya @ Nimbus): the Publish item shows only on own DRAFT rows (absent on an ACTIVE row); a complete draft → **ACTIVE** with `postedAt` refreshed to the publish moment + **quota 0→1**; an incomplete draft (no dept/city) → **400 "missing required fields: department, city"** shown in the dialog, stays DRAFT, **quota NOT consumed** (still 1); the atomic path re-verified after the review fixes; every fixture created was **deleted — DB restored to exactly 7 jobs**; **0 console errors**. (The transient Redis quota counter now reads 2/5 daily · 2/30 monthly from the two published-then-deleted fixtures — a TTL-reconciling counter, not DB job data; matches prior verification PRs that published/deleted jobs.)

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** a DRAFT→ACTIVE publish is now possible, but **create(PUBLISH)'s DTO still leaves `primaryCityId`/`functionalAreaId`/`openings` optional** — the wizard's Publish button gates them client-side, so via the UI you can't create an ACTIVE job without them, but a direct API `create({publishMode:'PUBLISH', title, description})` would produce a live job missing city/department/openings. `publish()` is deliberately stricter; tightening `CreateRecruiterJobDto` (a `publishMode==='PUBLISH'` refine) is a separate task. The best-effort quota refund is not transactional with the Redis counter (a double Redis failure could briefly leave a slot leaked until TTL) — accepted, matching the codebase's existing quota-reconciliation posture.

### `feature/recruiter-draft-jobs` — recruiter Draft Jobs nav sub-item + Save-as-Draft fix · 2026-07-17

**Recruiter "Draft Jobs" nav sub-item + Save-as-Draft fix** (SRS §4.9). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **Recruiter-only** — `apps/web` (job-seeker) + `apps/services` **byte-untouched** (`git diff develop…HEAD -- apps/web apps/services` empty); **no schema/migration/feature-flag/shared-surface-lock**. Two files changed: `SidebarNav.tsx` + `PostJobWizard.tsx`.

**Nav (Draft Jobs):** the sidebar **"Jobs"** entry — previously a flat top-level link — became a **collapsible disclosure group** matching the existing Settings/Billing/Help pattern (parent button toggles + highlights on any jobs route; children are the real destinations). Children: **"All jobs"** → `/jobs` and **"Draft Jobs"** → `/jobs?status=DRAFT`. Rather than build a new page, **Draft Jobs reuses the existing, fully-featured Jobs list** (the list already parses `?status=DRAFT` and the filter bar already has a Draft option), so drafts get the same filter bar, sorting, pagination and per-row ⋮ actions (Preview/Edit/Duplicate/Delete) for free — and it stays zero-duplication. `SidebarNav` gained `useSearchParams` (safe: it renders only in the `force-dynamic` authed layout) for **query-aware active state** — "Draft Jobs" is `aria-current` only when `?status=DRAFT` is present, "All jobs" only otherwise, so exactly one child highlights at a time while the parent stays highlighted across `/jobs` + `/jobs/*`. `TOP_ITEMS` was split into `TOP_ITEMS_ABOVE` (Dashboard) + `TOP_ITEMS_BELOW` (Post a Job/Profile/Verification/Users) with the Jobs group rendered between; `JOBS_ITEMS` added.

**Save-as-Draft fix:** the wizard's **"Save as draft"** button was `disabled={!titleOk}` and the code claimed "a draft only needs a title", but the create API's `CreateRecruiterJobDto` requires `description` (min 10) for **every** `publishMode` (DRAFT included) and `Job.description` is a **non-nullable** column — so saving a title-only draft POSTed `description: ''` and **400'd**. Fixed by requiring a description for drafts too (`disabled={!titleOk || !descriptionOk}`) with corrected helper copy (a description-needed hint when title-only; the publish hint only once a description exists) and an accurate comment explaining why (aligning the UI to the tested API contract — the alternative, a nullable-`description` migration, would ripple into `apps/web` rendering, which is off-limits). No API/schema/DTO change.

**Gate (integrated, develop @ 304da3c):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API 596 / web 186 / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19 — unchanged; recruiter-only change, and the recruiter app has no unit-test harness, consistent with every prior recruiter PR) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, demo recruiter Priya @ Nimbus): Jobs group expands to **All jobs + Draft Jobs**; "Draft Jobs" navigates to `/jobs?status=DRAFT` with the Status filter preselected to **Draft** and correct `aria-current` (and "All jobs" `aria-current` on the plain list); the draft button stays **disabled with a title only** (+ the new "Add a description…" hint) and **enables once a ≥10-char description is present**; saving created a real **DRAFT** (API accepted `publishMode=DRAFT`) that appeared under Draft Jobs (1 job) with a Draft badge and the correct ⋮ matrix (Preview/Edit/Duplicate/Delete — no Close/Reopen/Share); the fixture was then **deleted via the ⋮ menu → confirm dialog, restoring the DB to exactly 7 jobs**; **0 console errors**. (Screenshots timed out — the known browser-pane glitch; visuals verified via DOM/computed-style measurements.)

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** a saved DRAFT **cannot currently be published** through any recruiter action — the edit page is a PATCH that never changes status, and `close`/`reopen` explicitly reject DRAFT — so today a draft can only be edited/duplicated/deleted, never moved to ACTIVE (a DRAFT→ACTIVE "Publish" flow with quota consume + publish-field validation + ES sync + email was scoped out to keep this change small; flagged as a separate task). "Draft Jobs" via `?status=DRAFT` shows the four candidate-count columns as zeros (a draft has no applicants) — honest but noise; a drafts-tailored table was not pursued.

### `feature/recruiter-jobs-action-menu` — recruiter Jobs list 3-dot action menu · 2026-07-17

**Recruiter Jobs `/jobs` per-row ⋮ action menu** (SRS §4.9): Preview · Edit · Close · Delete · Duplicate · View Public Job Page · Share on Social Media (+ Reopen, kept — it existed before). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **Recruiter-only** — `apps/web` + `apps/services` **byte-untouched** (`git diff develop...HEAD -- apps/web apps/services` empty); **no schema/migration**. Packages touched additively only: `packages/ui/src/icons.ts` (+6 icons, append-only, no lock per §15.3), `packages/feature-flags/src/keys.ts` (+`KILL_RECRUITER_JOB_DELETE`, under the shared-surface lock, taken + released this merge), `packages/db/prisma/seed/flags.ts` (+1 seed row ⇒ **39 flags**), `.env.example` (+`NEXT_PUBLIC_WEB_URL`, a pre-existing doc gap).

**The menu** (`JobRowMenu`): hand-rolled `role=menu` on the **portalled Radix Popover** (NotificationBell pattern — **no new dependency**; the portal escapes the table's `overflow-x-auto` clip). Roving ArrowUp/Down/Home/End focus, **Space activates links too** (no native anchor Space), Tab/Escape close with focus return, `aria-haspopup="menu"` + `aria-expanded` on the trigger, `role="menu"` set **on the Radix content itself** (overrides its `role="dialog"`), focus-visible **inset 2px ring** (WCAG 2.4.7), disabled items stay focusable per APG (`aria-disabled` + hint line). Desktop: Actions column; mobile: card top-right; both verified.

**Item matrix by status/ownership** (owner-approved): *own rows* — Preview (DRAFT/PENDING_MODERATION) **or** View public job page (ACTIVE/EXPIRED/CLOSED) → `{WEB_URL}/job/<canonicalSlug>` new tab (the seeker page renders unpublished jobs as 200+noindex+banner — zero `apps/web` changes); Share (ACTIVE only) → `ShareJobDialog` with LinkedIn/X/WhatsApp web intents + clipboard copy-link; Edit; Duplicate; Close (ACTIVE/EXPIRED) / Reopen (CLOSED/EXPIRED) via the new `JobStatusDialog` (the app's canonical confirm pattern — **`window.confirm` + the silent-failure `JobActions.tsx` retired**; reopening an EXPIRED job now warns its past expiry date still applies); Delete (danger, disabled with a "close it instead" hint when the job has applications). *Teammate rows* — reduced read-only menu (Preview/View public/Share) since every mutation is own-jobs-only at the API.

**Delete** (owner decisions 2026-07-16): **hard delete only when the job has zero applications** — `Application`/`SavedJob` cascade on Job delete, and candidate application history must never be destroyed; jobs with responses must be closed instead. New `DELETE /recruiter/jobs/:id`: ownership 404 → **atomic single-statement `deleteMany({ id, postedById, applications: { none: {} } })`** (an application arriving mid-request can't be cascade-destroyed) → 409 w/ guidance (or 404 if concurrently deleted) → ES de-index + cache purge. **Three layers on `killswitch.recruiter_job_delete`** (seeded OFF ⇒ live): L3 503, L2 menu-item hidden (no L1 — free-feature precedent). Verified live: killswitch ON → 503 + item gone; direct API DELETE on a job with 14 applications → 409.

**Edit**: new `/jobs/[id]/edit` RSC (ownership/int4-guarded 404) reusing `PostJobWizard` in a new **edit mode** — single "Save changes" → the previously **UI-orphaned `PATCH /recruiter/jobs/:id`**; no publishMode/status/jobType sent (status stays on close/reopen; jobType keeps its purchased value); quota not consulted; company "Change" link hidden. **`UpdateRecruiterJobDto` now accepts `null` = clear** on the clearable optional fields (blank salary/area/etc. actually clears — omitted still = unchanged) **+ gained the same null-aware min≤max salary/experience refines create has** (an inverted range was storable via PATCH). Catalogue prefetch extracted to `lib/jobs/catalogues.ts` (shared with `/post-job`); the template-prefill mapping extracted to `lib/jobs/wizard-values.ts`.

**Duplicate**: `/post-job?duplicate=<id>` — the post-job page parses the param and `PostJobFlow` auto-runs its existing template deep-copy (starts on the template stage, no start-screen flash), landing on the prefilled form with the "copied" banner. Inherits the post-job killswitch + publish quota naturally; copies deliberately land on FREE/INTERNSHIP (never a paid jobType — the selector's gating isn't sidestepped).

**Adversarial review** (3 lenses — correctness/security, frontend/react/a11y, regression/isolation; every finding independently verified by a refuter agent): **29 raised → 26 confirmed (15 unique after cross-lens dedup), 1 refuted, all 15 fixed & re-verified.** Highlights: (HIGH) menu items suppressed the focus outline → focus-visible inset ring; (HIGH) danger text 4.0:1 on the popover → darkened via local `color-mix` (theme.css untouched, NotificationBell precedent); (MED) inverted salary/experience storable via PATCH → DTO refines; (MED) editing an internship posted as Part-time silently rewrote it to INTERN → prefill honoured; (LOW) network-failure spinner hang in all three dialogs → `api()` now returns `{ok:false,status:0}` on fetch rejection (also fixes the pre-existing RemoveUserDialog); (LOW) edit `/jobs/1.5/edit` 500 → int4 guard 404; (LOW) delete race misleading 409 → disambiguated 404; (LOW) edit prefill fabricated `openings=1` on null → stays unset; (LOW) blanked required-for-publish field silently kept old value → save blocked with hint; plus Space activation, `role=dialog`→`role=menu`, disabled-hint legibility, TemplatePicker error visibility with zero past jobs, EXPIRED-reopen expiry warning, duplicate start-flash.

**Gate (integrated, develop @ c363d6b):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API **596** (+12) / web 186 / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19 — the recruiter app has no unit-test harness, consistent with every prior recruiter PR) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, demo recruiter Priya @ Nimbus; every fixture created was deleted — **DB restored to exactly 7 jobs, original posters/titles/statuses, flag OFF**): full menu matrix per status (ACTIVE/DRAFT/CLOSED) incl. disabled-Delete hint; keyboard nav (arrows/Home/End/Escape/focus-return, focus-visible ring measured); share intent URLs correctly encoded + `noopener`; edit round-trip cross-checked vs Postgres (title changed → reverted; slug/status untouched); duplicate deep-link → prefilled form → draft created → **deleted via the dialog** (Postgres: gone); close→reopen cycle via dialogs (Postgres: CLOSED→ACTIVE); direct-API delete on a job with applications → **409**; killswitch ON → **L3 503 + L2 item hidden**, then restored OFF; teammate row (temp poster reassignment, restored) → reduced menu only; edit-page guards (teammate/float/big id) → **404×3**; mobile 375px (menu fits viewport, 32px trigger, no horizontal scroll); **0 console errors**.

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** reopen still doesn't reset a past `expiresAt` (pre-existing API; the dialog now warns on EXPIRED — an "extend on reopen" flow offered, not taken); a hand-rolled one-sided PATCH can still invert a range against the stored other side (service-level merged validation offered — the form always sends both sides); paid `jobType` still lacks L3 flag enforcement (pre-existing; duplicate deliberately downgrades to FREE); DRAFT/PENDING jobs remain world-readable at their public URL (pre-existing `apps/web` behaviour Preview leans on — a seeker-side gate would be an `apps/web` follow-up); local-dev seeker job pages 500 for companies with logos (pre-existing `next/image` allowlist gap for `localhost:4000`, surfaced during verification — `apps/web` untouchable here); recruiter module-permissions (`jobs` EDIT/READ_ONLY) remain display-only per the documented owner-approved deferral.

### `feature/recruiter-jobs-sort-pagination` — recruiter Jobs list sorting + full pagination · 2026-07-16

**Recruiter Jobs `/jobs` sorting + pagination controls** (SRS §4.9). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **Recruiter-only** — `apps/web` + `apps/services` + `packages` **byte-untouched** (`git diff develop...HEAD -- apps/web apps/services packages` empty); **no schema/migration/feature-flag/shared-surface-lock**. No API change (the page reads Postgres directly in the RSC — reads/writes split).

**Sorting:** the Job Title / Date Posted / Status table headers are now **clickable sort controls** — an inactive column applies its natural initial direction (dates newest-first, text/status ascending), the active column flips direction; a chevron shows the active direction (inactive headers preview theirs on hover) and the `<th>` carries `aria-sort` (computed server-side from the same shared parser the client links use, so the two can't drift). Below `md` the card layout gets a dedicated **Sort-by select** (6 options). URL convention matches the repo: direction baked into the value (`posted_asc`, `title_asc`, `title_desc`, `status_asc`, `status_desc`), default `posted_desc` omitted, every sort change deletes `page`. The Prisma `ORDER_BY` map ends every ordering in the unique `id` (companies-directory rule → no duplicated/dropped rows at page seams); **status sorts by the enum's declaration/lifecycle order** (Draft → Pending review → Open → Expired → Closed — an "Open-first" custom order would need raw SQL; accepted + documented).

**Pagination:** the bare Newer/Older nav became **numbered page links with ellipsis windowing** (≤7 pages shown in full, else `1 … window … last` — the design-system algorithm), Prev/Next chevrons, `aria-current="page"`, plus a **Results-per-page select (10 / 20 / 50)** — allow-listed server-side so `?perPage=` can never inflate the Prisma `take`; default 20 (and `page` 1) dropped from URLs. Changing page size resets to page 1. **Over-range `?page` now 307s to the real last page** (companies precedent; previously a confusing empty table), and `?page` is clamped to 1e6 so extreme values (`?page=1e19`) can't overflow Prisma's signed-64-bit `skip` into a 500 before the redirect runs (reproduced against the real client during review). The pagination bar and page list **flex-wrap** (WCAG 1.4.10 — no horizontal pane scroll down to 320px). `JobsFilterBar.clearAll` now deletes only the five filter keys, so **sort/per-page survive "Clear all filters"** (display preferences, not filters).

**New files** (`apps/recruiter/components/jobs/`): `jobs-list-params.ts` (shared sort/per-page parsers — the `applicant-filter.ts` pattern), `JobsSortHeader.tsx`, `JobsSortSelect.tsx`, `JobsPagination.tsx`. **Modified:** `jobs/page.tsx` (parsers + `ORDER_BY` + dynamic take/skip + over-range redirect + page clamp), `JobsTable.tsx` (`sort` prop + sortable `<th>`s), `JobsFilterBar.tsx` (`SELECT_CLASS` export + surgical `clearAll`). Old inline `PageLink` retired (file-local).

**Adversarial review** (3 lenses — correctness/security, frontend/react/a11y, regression/isolation — each finding independently verified): **6 raised (1 duplicate), 6 confirmed, 4 fixed & re-verified.** (MED) the pagination bar couldn't wrap → horizontal pane scroll at 320px (WCAG 1.4.10) and 480–580px → `flex-wrap` at both levels; (LOW) unclamped `?page=1e19` → client-side Prisma i64 `skip` overflow → 500 before the over-range redirect → clamp; (LOW) `aria-label` on the role-less disabled Prev/Next span is ARIA-prohibited (announced as a blank list item; axe `aria-prohibited-attr`) → `role="link"` + ellipsis `<li>` `aria-hidden`; (LOW) the mobile sort select used `router.replace` while the desktop headers are `<Link>`s (push) → Back left the page on mobile only → `router.push`. The 1 remaining confirmed finding is **pre-existing and byte-identical before this branch** (typing within the 300ms debounce of "Clear all filters" can resurrect just-cleared filters on a slow round-trip) — deferred to its own follow-up task rather than widening this diff.

**Gate (integrated, develop @ 8acef35):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API 584 / web 186 / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19 — the recruiter app has no unit-test harness, consistent with every prior recruiter PR) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, demo recruiter Priya @ Nimbus; 75 clearly-marked temp fixture jobs created to exercise 82-job/9-page states, then **deleted — DB restored to exactly 7 jobs / 0 fixtures**): all three columns sorted both directions **cross-checked against Postgres — exact match including page-seam integrity**; ellipsis windowing at page 5 of 9; per-page switching (default dropped from URL); `?page=99` **and** `?page=1e19` land on the real last page; filter+sort+per-page interplay incl. Clear-all; mobile sort select + Back-button semantics; empty states show no orphaned controls; **0 console errors**; **no horizontal pane scroll at 320 / 375 / 520 / 780 / 1280** (the 780px document-level `scrollWidth` delta is the table's own pre-existing designed internal card scroll). Screenshots timed out (the known browser-pane glitch) — visuals verified via DOM/computed-style measurements.

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** the pre-existing clearAll/debounce race (flagged as its own task with a verifier-suggested fix); title sort follows the DB collation (case-sensitive-ish ordering, e.g. "SRE" before "Senior" — deterministic and acceptable); an "Open-first" status order would need raw SQL (enum lifecycle order accepted); the identical ARIA-prohibited-label flaw exists in `apps/web`'s `SrpPaginationLink` but that app must stay byte-untouched here (candidate for a seeker-side follow-up).

### `feature/recruiter-jobs-candidate-counts` — recruiter Jobs list candidate-count columns · 2026-07-12

**Recruiter Jobs `/jobs` candidate-count columns** (SRS §4.9). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **Recruiter-only** — `apps/web` (job-seeker) + `apps/services` **byte-untouched** (`git diff develop…HEAD -- apps/web apps/services` empty); **no schema/migration/feature-flag/shared-surface-lock**. No API change (the page reads Postgres directly in an RSC — reads/writes split).

**What shipped:** each job row on the Jobs list now shows four **clickable** candidate-count columns so a recruiter can gauge activity without opening the posting: **Responses** (all applications for the job) · **New** (`status = APPLIED`, i.e. not yet actioned) · **Shortlisted** (`status = SHORTLISTED`) · **Matches** (applicants to *this* job whose `Candidate.skillIds` overlap the job's `skillIds`; 0 when the job lists no required skills). Definitions confirmed with the owner up front (New = APPLIED, not a new "viewed" flag → no migration; Matches = matched **applicants**, not a Resdex-style whole-DB search).

**Data layer:** computed server-side in the Jobs-page RSC — a single `prisma.application.groupBy(['jobId','status'])` over the page's ≤20 job ids yields Responses/New/Shortlisted; **Matches** is a bounded set of per-job `prisma.application.count` queries (skipped for jobs with no required skills), run in parallel. The `[jobId, status]` index keeps this cheap. The old single `_count.applications` "Applicants" column is replaced.

**Clickability / applicants filter:** each number links to `/jobs/[id]/applicants` with a new **additive `?filter=new|shortlisted|matched`** param (Responses → no filter). The applicants page parses it (via a shared `applicant-filter.ts` narrower — the same one the tabs use), applies it to both the `findMany` and the `count`, shows filter-aware header text ("5 new" / "3 matches" / …) + a filter-aware empty state, and preserves the filter across pagination. A new client **`ApplicantsFilterTabs`** (All/New/Shortlisted/Matches, mirroring the existing sort toggle) makes the deep-link discoverable + reversible.

**Ownership gating:** the Jobs list is company-wide but the applicants page is owner-private (404s for teammates' jobs). So metric numbers render as **links only for the recruiter's own jobs**; teammate rows — and any **zero** count — render as plain text (no 404-bound links, no dead clicks). Consistent with the existing `JobLink` view-only pattern.

**Layout (owner-approved decision):** four extra columns overflowed the recruiter shell's `max-w-3xl` (768px) form-oriented content cap, producing a **real horizontal *page* scrollbar** at ≤~1279px. Rather than cram 9 columns or widen the whole portal, the Jobs page **opts into the wider content column** (`data-wide` root → authed layout `has-[[data-wide]]:max-w-6xl`), and the root `<body>` clips document-level horizontal overflow **only for wide pages** (`has-[[data-wide]]:overflow-x-clip`) — the table still scrolls inside its own `overflow-x-auto` card, and every other recruiter page keeps the narrow reading width. The table fully fits at ≥1280px; below that its card scrolls (a focusable `role="region"` so it's keyboard-reachable). Mobile shows the four metrics as a 2×2 tile grid (4-across ≥480px).

**New files** (`apps/recruiter/components/jobs/`): `applicant-filter.ts` (shared filter keys/labels/parser), `ApplicantsFilterTabs.tsx`. **Modified:** `jobs/page.tsx` (metric computation + `skillIds` select + row mapping), `JobsTable.tsx` (4 metric columns desktop + mobile tile grid, owner/zero gating), `jobs/[id]/applicants/page.tsx` (`?filter=` + header/empty + pagination), `ApplicantsTable.tsx` (filter-aware `emptyTitle` prop), `(authed)/layout.tsx` (wide opt-in), `app/layout.tsx` (scoped body overflow-x clip).

**Adversarial review** (3 lenses — correctness/security, frontend/react/a11y, regression/isolation — each finding independently verified): **7 raised, 3 confirmed & fixed**, then re-verified in the browser. (MED) zero counts used `--color-fg-subtle`, which fails WCAG AA → switched to AA-passing `--color-fg-muted` (desktop cells + mobile tiles). (LOW) `ApplicantsFilterTabs` highlighted the raw `?filter=` value, so an invalid value left no tab active while the server showed All → now narrows via the shared `parseApplicantFilter`. (NIT) the horizontal-scroll table region wasn't keyboard-focusable → `role="region"` + `tabIndex=0` (WCAG 2.1.1). 4 findings correctly refuted (Total including WITHDRAWN matches prior behaviour; two sequential count round-trips; mobile label overflow; `role=tab` vs `aria-current`).

**Gate (integrated, develop @ 74179ad):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API 584 / web 186 / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19 — the recruiter app has no unit-test harness, consistent with every prior recruiter PR) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, demo recruiter Priya @ Nimbus, 7 jobs): the four counts **cross-checked against Postgres for all 7 jobs — exact match**, including the skill-overlap Matches logic; every column deep-links to the correctly-filtered applicants list (New→7 APPLIED, Matches→3 mixed-status skill-overlap, Shortlisted→0 empty state); invalid `?filter=bogus` falls back to "All" (14 rows); zero counts render as non-link plain text (DOM-asserted); **no horizontal page scroll at 375 / 1024 / 1280 / 1440** (`hScrollbar=0`; table fully fits at ≥1280, card-scrolls below); mobile 2×2 grid with no label clipping; **0 console errors**. (Screenshots timed out — a browser-pane tooling glitch; visuals verified via DOM/computed-style measurements.)

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** "New" is APPLIED-status, not a true "viewed by recruiter" flag (that would need a schema migration + a mark-as-viewed write path — offered, not taken). "Matches" is matched **applicants**, not a whole-database Resdex/Reach-style search (heavier, arguably a paid surface). Teammate rows stay view-only (opening a teammate's filtered applicants would require broadening the owner-only applicants page — a candidate-PII/RBAC decision kept out of scope, as in the prior Jobs-filters PR).

### `feature/srp-expanding-search` — SRP click-to-expand search · 2026-07-12

**Click-to-expand search on the SRP** `/jobs` (+ all `[...path]` SEO landings via `SrpShell`) — SRS §4.1. CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). `apps/web` only — no schema/migration/feature-flag/shared-surface-lock.

**What shipped:** a new client component `SrpSearchExpand` (`components/srp`) replaces the compact `SearchInput` in the SRP header. At rest it renders a trigger styled exactly like the current segmented `withButton` search bar (search icon + current query + navy "Search" button). Clicking it **pops out** — opacity 0→1 + scale 0.97→1, 200ms ease-out, elevated as a popover (`--shadow-float`) — into the **home hero's three-field `HeroSearchBar`** (skills / location / experience + Search), **prefilled with the current query** (`initialWhat`) and its first field auto-focused. It collapses on **outside mousedown / Escape** (Escape returns focus to the trigger) **/ a new search** (the component is keyed by `searchQuery`, so it remounts collapsed). The popover is **always mounted** and toggled with the `inert` attribute + opacity/scale, so the motion is smooth in both directions and its fields stay out of the tab order + off screen readers while shut. It **pops out wider** (`max-w-4xl` ≈ 896px) than the 768px resting bar so the three fields don't clip their placeholders (and it sells the effect). `HeroSearchBar` gained optional `initial{What,Where,Exp}` props (default empty → the home hero is byte-unchanged); `SrpShell` swaps `SearchInput` → `SrpSearchExpand` and drops the `max-w-3xl` wrapper (the trigger now owns its width).

**Gate:** typecheck **11/11** · tests **API 584 + web 186** · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified**: rest state (trigger + inert/opacity-0 panel); open (panel opacity target 1, scale-100, inert removed, pointer-events auto, prefilled "react" focused, 869px wider popover, placeholders fit); outside-mousedown + collapse (back to inert/opacity-0); mobile 375px stacks; zero horizontal overflow at 1320px + 375px; console clean (no React `inert` warning).

**Implementation note:** an early `requestAnimationFrame` guard for the enter transition was removed — the always-mounted popover is painted at opacity-0 for many frames before the click, so the opacity transition fires cleanly on toggle without rAF. (The headless preview runs as a *hidden* tab, which pauses rAF + freezes CSS transitions; the animation *end-states* were verified via kill-transition and the motion runs in a real visible tab.)

**Adversarial review** (completed just after the first merge → fixed in a **fast-follow** on this branch): **13 raised, 9 confirmed, 6 fixed, 1 accepted.** (MED) collapse-on-submit relied only on the `key={searchQuery}` remount, so a **same-`q` / city-only search left the popover floating open** over the results → added an `onSearch` callback so `HeroSearchBar` collapses it on **every** submit (browser-verified: a same-`q` submit now closes it); (MED, a11y) the open trigger stayed **focusable + in the AT tree** (only `opacity-0`) so Shift-Tab landed on an invisible button → the trigger is now **`inert` when open**, and the Escape focus-return moved to a **post-render effect** (focusing an inert node is a no-op); (LOW) the empty-state trigger label was `fg-subtle` **2.6:1** → `fg-muted` **7.79:1** AA; (NIT) panel radius `rounded-full` → **`rounded-2xl sm:rounded-full`** to match the inner form on mobile; (LOW) dropped the two **unused `initialWhere/Exp`** props. **Accepted (NIT):** one Escape collapses both an inner suggestion dropdown and the popover (defensible "Escape = get out"). **Other notes:** the rest-state trigger's "Search" button expands rather than submits (consistent with clicking anywhere on the bar); the popover uses `HeroSearchBar`'s curated role list rather than the old live `/api/search/suggest` typeahead — an accepted trade for reusing the exact hero search.

### `feature/home-hero-copy` — home hero rework · 2026-07-12

**Reworked the home hero** — headline, subtext, colour, size, layout, and search bar (SRS §4.1). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). `apps/web` only — two files (`components/home/Hero.tsx` + `HeroSearchBar.tsx`); no schema/migration/feature-flag/shared-surface-lock. Iterated live with the owner over several rounds.

**What shipped:**
- **Headline** → **"Where great careers begin."** — the word "careers" is emphasised in **flat brand cyan** (`--color-accent-600`, `#0087be`, 4.03:1 AA; the exact brand `accent-500`/`#22A0DA` measured 2.95:1, just under the 3:1 large-text floor). The old `gradient-text` keyword is **removed** (owner banned gradients). Display size reduced from up-to-**72px → 48px** (`text-3xl sm:text-4xl lg:text-5xl`) at the owner's request ("too big").
- **Subtext** → **"Real jobs, real companies. No ads."** (honest trust signals, no fabricated stat — the owner's "5 lakh+" reference would have been false against the demo data).
- **Background de-gradiented** — removed the navy→cyan `--gradient-mesh` layer + the cyan aurora blur; kept only the neutral dot texture (flat brand colours only, per the standing no-gradients preference).
- **Layout top-weighted** — the hero was a full-viewport (`min-h-[calc(100svh-72px)]`) vertically-centred block; it's now **content-driven** (no forced height) so shifting the content up doesn't leave a large empty void beneath it.
- **`HeroSearchBar`** — the segmented search bar is **wider** (`max-w-3xl`→`max-w-4xl`, 768→896px), input labels are **16px semibold** (were 14px regular), and the "what" field was widened relative to "location" so the full placeholder fits without clipping.

**Gate (integrated with `develop` incl. companies-redesign + recruiter-jobs-filters):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **API 584 + web 186** · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** at 1320px + 375px (single-line headline at both widths, cyan accent AA, wider search bar, no horizontal overflow, console clean).

**Deferred / follow-ups:** the header/navbar and the `/companies` redesign shipped on their own branches; this branch is hero-only.

### `feature/companies-directory-redesign` — full `/companies` seeker directory redesign · 2026-07-12

**Full redesign of the job-seeker companies directory** `/companies` (SRS §4.7). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). `apps/web` only — **no schema/migration/feature-flag**; the only shared surfaces are the `components/companies/index.ts` barrel (append-only, then reverted to keep the client components deep-imported) + a one-line stale-comment fix in `components/shell/SiteShell.tsx`.

**Architectural note:** the page was a bare `<main>` with `revalidate = 3600` (edge ISR). It's now wrapped in `SiteShell`, whose header/footer resolve auth **server-side** (cookies via `getHeaderUser`), so the route renders **dynamically** like the rest of the public site (home / SRP / job-detail) and `revalidate` was dropped — a personalised header can't be shared-edge-cached. (Recorded the same on `SiteShell`'s doc comment, which had gone stale claiming client-side auth.)

**What shipped:**
- **Shared chrome** — the directory now carries the real `SiteHeader` + `SiteFooter` (was headerless/footerless).
- **`IndustryShowcase`** (new client component) — a horizontally-scrollable "browse by industry" rail of category cards on a faint brand-navy band (`primary-50`), each showing the **real** per-industry company count (a `company.groupBy(industryId)`) in brand cyan, linking to the filtered directory. Scroll arrows appear only on overflow (kept mounted+disabled at the edges so keyboard focus is never dropped); touch/keyboard scroll everywhere. No fabricated numbers.
- **`CompanyFilters`** (new client component) — an elevated faceted-filter **card** in a sticky sidebar: **Sort** (Top rated / Most reviewed / A–Z) + **Availability** (Currently hiring) + **Industry**, single-select groups as radio rows, hiring as a checkbox, an iconed header with a navy **active-filter count badge** + Clear all, and count pills. All URL-driven `<Link>`s (no client state). Renders **bare** inside the reused `MobileFilterSheet` on mobile (no card-in-card).
- **Redesigned `CompanyCard`** — **"The Ledger"** data-console tile, selected via a 4-concept design judge-panel. Signature = a hairline-divided semantic `<dl>` of the three facts a seeker scans (**Open roles / Rating / Location**), big tabular values baseline-aligned over whisper uppercase labels on a flat navy tint; a bold headline with a navy **kicker-tick** that widens on hover; a cyan **"Hiring"** tag; a ghost **"View jobs"** affordance. Whole card is one `<Link>` with a composed aria-label; flat brand colours only, one hover-lift, strict AA. Amber star reuses `RatingStars`' oklch value (no new token).
- **`lib/companies/params.ts`** — gains `sort` (`rating`|`name`|`reviews`) + `hiring` (bool) parsing and `buildDirectoryQuery` (drops defaults, preserves filters across pagination). Page adds a deterministic `{ id: 'asc' }` orderBy tiebreaker and an **over-range `?page` → last-page 307 redirect**. +12 params unit tests (web now 186).

**Adversarial review** (4 lenses — correctness / SSR-boundary / a11y / responsive-brand, each finding verified): **15 raised, 14 fixed, 1 accepted** (per-industry counts are intentionally unfiltered totals, documented). Fixes incl. the page-clamp redirect, deterministic pagination, sort-neutral "Previous/Next" labels, showcase links preserving `sort`+`hiring`, `aria-current` (not invalid `aria-pressed`) on the hiring toggle, `fg-subtle`→`fg-muted` on meaningful text (2.57→7.79:1 AA), active showcase border `accent-500`→`accent-600` (3:1 non-text floor), scroll-arrows kept mounted, `sr-only` results heading, and matching the SRP's `top-20` sticky offset.

**Gate (integrated with the teammate's `feature/recruiter-jobs-filters`):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **API 584 + web 186** · build **4/4** apps (`/companies` builds `ƒ` dynamic). **Browser-verified** at 1320px + 375px + **320px** (industry rail + data-console cards + filter card, filtering end-to-end, mobile sheet bare/single-title, composed aria-label, zero horizontal overflow, console clean).

**Deferred / follow-ups:** an "Open roles"-based sort would need a job-count aggregation for ordering (not a column) — left out; per-industry facet counts don't reflect the active `hiring` filter (intentional browsing aid); live hover micro-interactions couldn't be measured in the headless preview (standard app-wide `group-hover` pattern, verified structurally).

### `feature/recruiter-jobs-filters` — recruiter Jobs list Filters & Search bar · 2026-07-12

**Recruiter Jobs `/jobs` Filters & Search bar** (SRS §4.9). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **Recruiter-only** — `apps/web` (job-seeker) + `apps/services` **byte-untouched** (`git diff develop…HEAD -- apps/web apps/services` empty); **no schema/migration/feature-flag/shared-surface-lock**. No API change (the page reads Postgres directly in an RSC — reads/writes split).

**What shipped:** a new `JobsFilterBar` client component above the table — a **debounced search** matching **job title (case-insensitive substring) OR an exact numeric Job ID**, plus four filter dropdowns: **Status** (reuses `JOB_STATUS_META` labels), **Category** (the `jobType` enum: Free · Hot Vacancy · SMB · Internship), **Location** (the company's distinct job cities), and **Posted by** (teammates who've posted). Everything is **URL/searchParams-driven** (mirroring the retired `JobsStatusFilter`), so it composes with the existing server-side pagination — `PageLink` now preserves every active param (previously only `page`+`status`). A "Clear all filters" affordance appears when any filter is active.

**Company-wide scope (owner-approved decision):** to give **Posted by** teammates to filter on, the Jobs list was broadened from the recruiter's own postings (`where.postedById = session.sub`) to **company-wide** (`where.companyId`, resolved via `prisma.recruiter.findUnique`). Because the applicants page + close/reopen API remain **own-jobs-only** (`getOne` 404s when `postedById !== session.sub`), teammate rows are **view-only**: a new `JobLink` helper renders the title + applicant-count as **plain text (not links)** for teammates, and `RowActions` (Close/Reopen) is `isOwn`-gated — so no teammate row ever shows a 404-bound link or a button that can't succeed. Own rows are unchanged.

**New files** (`apps/recruiter/components/jobs/`): `JobsFilterBar.tsx` (the bar; native `appearance-none` selects + custom chevron so the token background/foreground own the control in both themes). **Modified:** `jobs/page.tsx` (company scope + `readSearch`/`readCategory`/`readIntId` param parsers + `where` + two `distinct` option-list queries scoped to the whole company so dropdowns don't collapse + `PageLink` param preservation + per-row `isOwn` + a `role="status"` count), `JobsTable.tsx` (`isOwn` on `JobListRow` + `JobLink` helper + `isOwn`-gated actions), `job-list-format.ts` (`JOB_TYPE_LABELS`/`JobCategory`). **Removed:** `JobsStatusFilter.tsx` (folded into the bar; nothing else imported it).

**Adversarial review** (3 dimensions — correctness/security, frontend/react/a11y, regression/isolation — each finding independently verified): **6 raised, all 6 confirmed & fixed**, then re-verified in the browser. (HIGH) teammate title/applicant-count links pointed at the own-jobs-only applicants page → 404 → gated via `JobLink`. (MED) the debounced search commit closed over a stale `searchParams` snapshot, so a dropdown change made mid-debounce was silently dropped → `setParam` now reads the latest params via a ref. (MED) the URL→input sync effect clobbered the search box mid-typing (stripped trailing spaces, jumped the caret) → it now re-syncs only on genuine external changes (Clear all / back-forward) via a last-committed ref. (LOW) the result count wasn't a live region → `role="status"`. (LOW) a disabled select's custom chevron didn't dim → now dims in lockstep.

**Gate (integrated, develop @ d84e187):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (web 181; API 584 / auth 17 / feature-flags 37 / observability 19 unchanged & cached — the recruiter app has no unit-test harness, consistent with every prior recruiter PR) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, demo recruiter Priya @ Nimbus, 7 jobs): the bar renders all controls; Location (`Hyderabad` → 1); Job-ID search (`100005` → that job); title search (`Frontend` → that job); empty state (`status=DRAFT` → "No jobs match these filters"); a **temporary one-job poster reassignment** proved the multi-poster path (Posted-by lists both recruiters; filtering by each; the teammate row correctly shows **no Close action and plain-text title/count**) and the **company-wide scope** (the caller still sees a job posted by another user); combined AND filters + `page` reset; the debounced search preserves a concurrent dropdown change and keeps a trailing space while the URL trims it; Clear-all resets; 0 console errors. All temporary demo-data mutations were **restored** (job 100007 back to its original poster; Nimbus back to 7 jobs / 1 poster).

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** teammate rows are **view-only** — opening a teammate's applicants (and closing/reopening their jobs) would require broadening the applicants page + `RecruiterApplicantsService` + close/reopen authorization from owner-only to company-scope, a candidate-PII/RBAC decision deliberately kept out of this PR (offered as a follow-up). "Classified" from the original ask has no schema equivalent, so Category surfaces the real `jobType` values (all current jobs are Free; the paid types stay empty until such postings exist). The recruiter portal mounts no theme toggle (light-only in practice), though the bar renders correctly in dark too.

### `feature/navbar-brand-centering` — center CQ mark over wordmark, resize brand, shift nav right · 2026-07-12

**Navbar brand polish** (SRS §4.1). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). One code file — `apps/web/components/home/SiteHeader.tsx` (the shared `SiteHeader`, so it applies to every public page: home, `/jobs` + SEO SRPs, `/job/[slug]`, companies, career-advice). **No schema/migration/feature-flag/shared-surface-lock.** Owner asked for four things, all done and browser-measured:

- **Logo a little smaller** — the CQ mark went `h-10` (40px) → **`h-9` (36px)**.
- **Wordmark a little bigger** — "Career Queue" went `text-sm` (14px) → **`text-base` (16px)**.
- **Mark centred over the text, equal space L/R** — the brand `<Link>` flex column went **`items-start` → `items-center`** (centres the logo box over the wordmark), the eyeballed **`pl-1` was dropped**, and a **measured `-translate-x-[2px]`** was added to the mark. Root cause the old `pl-1` hack was papering over: the visible glyph is *not* centred inside its own PNG — the opaque pixels of `cq-mark-color.png` (400×178) run x=52→368, i.e. **52px transparent padding on the left vs 32px on the right**, so the glyph's optical centre sits +10px (≈2.5% of width, ~2px at h-9) right of the box centre. The −2px cancels that, so the **visible** glyph lands on the text centre (browser-verified: glyph-centre − text-centre = **0.02px**). Measured the PNG's alpha bbox with Pillow rather than guessing.
- **Nav links further right** — the `<nav>` gained **`lg:ml-6`**, taking the brand→nav gap **32px → 56px** (the links were reading as "too close" to the brand).

**Verified in-browser** at 1320px + 375px: bar still 72px with symmetric 7px above the logo / 7px below the text; nav vertically centred; **no horizontal overflow** at either width; **no console errors/hydration warnings**; the fix holds at mobile (nav correctly hidden below `lg`, mark still centred). **Adversarial multi-agent review** (3 lenses — design/correctness, regression/isolation, a11y/responsive/dark-mode — each finding adversarially verified): **0 confirmed** (2 sub-pixel NITs refuted: the dark-mode white mark is actually *slightly better* centred with the shared nudge and is never rendered since dark mode is dormant; the fixed-px translate is already documented at the call site and worst-case ~0.25px over a full height step). **Gate (integrated with the teammate's `feature/recruiter-jobs-list-table`):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **API 584 + web 181** · build **4/4** apps. **Note:** `SiteHeader` is imported only within `apps/web`; recruiter/services untouched. **Follow-up (offered, not taken):** the durable fix for the glyph asymmetry is re-cropping the mark PNG to symmetric padding (removes the height-coupled translate entirely) — deferred as a brand-asset change out of scope for this CSS tweak.

### `feature/recruiter-jobs-list-table` — recruiter Jobs list → structured table + color-coded status badges · 2026-07-12

**Recruiter `/jobs` list redesign** (SRS §4.9). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). **Recruiter-only** — `apps/web` (job-seeker) + `apps/services` **byte-untouched** (`git diff develop -- apps/web apps/services` empty); **no schema/migration/feature-flag/shared-surface-lock**. No API change (the page reads Postgres directly in an RSC — the reads/writes split).

**What shipped:** the flat `JobRow` divider list on `/jobs` became a **structured table** — columns **Job Title · Location · Date Posted · Status**, plus **Applicants** and **Actions** (Close/Reopen preserved). Responsive: a real `<table>` at `md+`, stacked cards below. Each posting shows a **color-coded status tag** so state is identifiable at a glance: **Open (green) · Draft (grey) · Expired (amber) · Closed (red)** (plus a distinct navy "Pending review" for the normally-unreachable `PENDING_MODERATION`). A small dot + the text label mean state is never signalled by color alone (WCAG 1.4.1).

**New files** (`apps/recruiter/components/jobs/`): `JobStatusBadge.tsx` (the reusable color-coded badge + `JOB_STATUS_META` — the single source of truth for job-status label/variant), `JobsTable.tsx` (the table + mobile cards), `job-list-format.ts` (pure `formatJobLocation`/`formatListDate`). **Modified:** `jobs/page.tsx` (widened the RSC Prisma `select` for `primaryCity`/`locality`/`workMode` — additive read, no schema change — and renders `JobsTable`), `JobsStatusFilter.tsx` ("Active" pill → "Open"), `TemplatePicker.tsx` (**unified** onto the shared `JOB_STATUS_META`/`JobStatusBadge` — it had drifted: ACTIVE "Active" vs "Open", EXPIRED grey vs amber, PENDING "In review" vs "Pending review"). **Removed:** `JobRow.tsx` (superseded; nothing else imported it). Location renders as `Remote` / `{city} · Hybrid` / `{locality}, {city}` / `—`.

**Adversarial review** (3 dimensions → each finding independently verified): **correctness clean (0)**. 5 confirmed a11y findings; **4 fixed**: (MED) the shared Badge `success`/`danger` foregrounds fail WCAG AA on their light pills (Open 2.65:1, Closed 3.61:1) → darkened **only** the Open/Closed foregrounds via a tailwind-merge className override **scoped to `JobStatusBadge`** (browser-verified **4.54:1 / 4.99:1**), leaving `packages/ui/Badge.tsx` byte-untouched so the seeker/services apps are unaffected; (MED) desktop applicant-count link had a bare-number accessible name → `aria-label`; (LOW) truncated titles → `title` tooltips; (LOW) small mobile tap target → padded. The 1 refuted finding: `<th>` without `scope` (not needed for a simple single-header-row table). The `TemplatePicker` divergence (5th finding) was resolved by the unification above (owner-requested, in the same PR).

**Gate (integrated, develop @ fe660cf):** typecheck **11/11** · tests **5/5** (API 584, web 181, auth 17, feature-flags 37, observability 19 — unchanged; the recruiter app has no unit-test harness, consistent with every prior recruiter PR) · build **4/4** apps. **Browser-verified** (real browser, demo recruiter Priya): the table renders all columns; a temporary Open/Draft/Expired/Closed data mix confirmed all four badge colors render **and pass AA** (computed sRGB contrast); location formatting (`Bangalore`, `Bangalore · Hybrid`, `Hyderabad · Hybrid`); actions gate correctly (Open→Close, Expired/Closed→Reopen, Draft→none); the status filter still works (`?status=EXPIRED` → 1 row); dark mode legible; **0 console errors**; and the `TemplatePicker` now shows the same "Open" (green, AA) badge + "Open" dropdown label. All temporary demo-data mutations were **restored** (7 jobs back to ACTIVE with exact original `expiresAt`).

**Deferred / follow-ups (by design):** the underlying shared-Badge `success`/`danger` AA gap is fixed only at the recruiter Jobs surface (the scoped override) — a proper design-system fix in `packages/ui/Badge.tsx` (which benefits every portal) is a separate, coordinated change needing the theme/UI review + an `apps/web` regression pass. No edit-existing-job screen was added (out of scope). Table row is not whole-row-clickable (title + count links + action buttons instead) — an accepted trade-off vs the seeker cards' stretched-link pattern.

### `feature/recruiter-post-a-job` — Phase 4 / final (rich JD editor + Salary Trends + Reach Meter) · 2026-07-09

**Post a Job revamp — Phase 4, the final phase** (SRS §4.9). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). Completes the four-phase feature. **On pull run `pnpm db:generate && pnpm db:migrate:dev`.**

**Schema:** additive migration `20260709130705_add_job_description_markdown` — `Job.descriptionMarkdown String?` (nullable; existing jobs keep the plain render → zero regression).

**Rich JD editor:** the Description field gained a **Bold / Bullets / Heading** Markdown toolbar (inserts Markdown around the selection — **no new dependency**). On submit the form sends `descriptionMarkdown` (rich source) plus a Markdown-stripped plain `description` (JSON-LD/search/legacy fallback). Template prefill carries `descriptionMarkdown` too.

**Seeker render — the one owner-approved `apps/web` touch, additive + zero-regression:** `JobBody` renders `descriptionMarkdown` through the **existing sanitized `renderArticleMarkdown` pipeline** (rehype-sanitize — reused, no new dep, no XSS) when present; when absent (every existing job) it uses the original plain-text path unchanged. Footprint = `JobBody.tsx` + one prop on `app/job/[slug]/page.tsx`. **`apps/services` byte-untouched.**

**Salary Trends** — read-only `GET /recruiter/jobs/salary-trends?title=&cityId=` benchmarks from our own ACTIVE postings (midpoint salaries matching title keyword + city → min/median/max LPA; null under 3 points) → sticky debounced sidebar panel. **Reach Meter** — `GET /recruiter/jobs/reach?skillIds=&cityId=&experienceMonths=` counts candidates overlapping skills + preferred city (+ min exp) → live "~N matching candidates" gauge (0 until skills/city set). Both recruiter-guarded, read-only, declared before `:id`. +4 service tests → **API 584**.

**Gate (integrated, develop @ c8f03de):** typecheck **11/11** · build **4/4** · tests **5/5** (API 584 (+4), web 181, auth 17, feature-flags 37, observability 19). **Browser-verified**: Salary Trends live ("Engineer" + Bangalore → 10 jobs, ₹18–77.5L, median ₹40L); Reach Meter live (Bangalore → 16, varies by city, 0 with no filters); Markdown toolbar inserts `## `/`**…**`/`- …`; the **seeker** renders a Markdown JD as sanitized `<h2>`/`<strong>`/`<ul><li>` with **0 raw markdown**, while a plain job renders **byte-identical** `whitespace-pre-line` paragraphs (zero-regression proof). No demo mutations remain (the demo logo + test markdown used for the check were restored). *The seeker dev server first 404'd (stale `.next` — cleared via `pnpm --filter @jobportal/web clean`, DEVELOPMENT.md §5) then 500'd on a pre-existing `next/image` localhost-logo dev config — both unrelated to this change; it verified cleanly once past them.*

**Feature complete.** All four phases merged: P1 nav + route move · P2 template picker + job-type selector · P3 rich form + schema · P4 rich JD editor + Salary Trends + Reach Meter. **Deferred (not required):** monthly-stipend semantics for internships (salary stays annual → no seeker change); Hot Vacancy/SMB per-type field differences + paid enforcement (stay flag-gated OFF); edit-existing-job UI (PATCH exists, no screen); admin-tunable job products; richer salary/reach models than own-pool aggregates.

### `feature/recruiter-post-a-job` — Phase 3 (rich Job Details form + schema) · 2026-07-09

**Post a Job revamp — Phase 3** (SRS §4.9). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). Recruiter-only; **`apps/web` + `apps/services` byte-untouched** (`git diff develop -- apps/web apps/services` empty). First schema change in the feature — **on pull run `pnpm db:generate && pnpm db:migrate:dev`.**

**Schema** (one additive migration `20260709122514_add_job_type_openings_locality`, verified `CREATE TYPE`/`ADD COLUMN`/`CREATE TABLE` only — no drops, so seeker reads + `JobPosting` JSON-LD are unaffected):
- `enum JobType { FREE HOT_VACANCY SMB INTERNSHIP }` + `Job.jobType JobType @default(FREE)` (backfills existing rows).
- `Job.openings Int?`, `Job.qualifications String?`, `Job.internshipDurationMonths Int?` (all nullable).
- `model Locality` (id/slug@unique/name/cityId→City Cascade/@@index) + `Job.localityId Int?` (SetNull) + `City.localities` back-relation.

**Seeds:** new `seed/functional-areas.ts` (**20 departments** — this fixed a latent bug: `FunctionalArea` was never seeded, so the wizard's Department dropdown had always been empty) + `seed/localities.ts` (**84 curated areas** across 14 major cities, city-prefixed slugs). Both wired into `seed.ts` after cities.

**API** (`recruiter-jobs`): `CreateRecruiterJobDto`/`UpdateRecruiterJobDto` gain `jobType`, `openings`, `qualifications`, `localityId`, `localityName`, `internshipDurationMonths`. The service now persists them and adds `resolveLocalityId()` — if a `localityId` is given it's validated to belong to the chosen city (400 otherwise); if a free-typed `localityName` is given it's find-or-created as a City-scoped `Locality` (slug `${citySlug}-${slug}`). **`jobType` is now persisted (the L3-trusted write).** +3 service tests → **API 580**.

**Form:** `PostJobWizard` rebuilt from the 6-step wizard into a **single-column sectioned Job Details form** per the SRS field list: **Job details** (Company auto-filled + Change→/profile · Department · Industry · Title · Full/Part/Contract toggle — or, for the Internship type, an Internship/Part-time toggle + Duration months · Number of openings) · **Location** (City → **Area/Locality dependent dropdown**, filtered by the chosen city, with an "Other (type an area)…" free-text fallback · Work mode) · **Candidate requirements** (Total experience Any/Fresher/Experienced — Experienced reveals min/max years · Key skills chip-picker · Qualifications & must-haves) · **Salary** (min/max LPA) · **Description** (short + long textarea — the rich editor is Phase 4) · **Save as draft / Publish job** with mandatory-field gating for Publish. Template prefill (`PostJobFlow`) extended to carry the new fields.

**Gate (integrated, develop @ 1f67a7c):** typecheck **11/11** · build **4/4** · tests **5/5** (API 580 (+3), web 181, auth 17, feature-flags 37, observability 19). **Browser-verified end-to-end** (real browser, demo recruiter): all 5 sections render; Department dropdown now populated (20); City→Area dependent dropdown (Bangalore → 10 areas + "Other"); a real draft save persisted **all** new fields to the DB (`jobType=FREE`, `openings=1`, `qualifications`, `functionalAreaId`→dept, `localityId`→Koramangala, city Bangalore); Internship variant shows Duration + hides the Full/Part toggle; zero console errors; test job deleted (no demo mutations).

**Deferred to Phase 4 (final):** the rich-text description editor (sanitized HTML) + the one owner-approved additive `apps/web/JobBody` render tweak; the Salary-Trends benchmark panel; the live Reach-Meter. Also deferred (not required): monthly-stipend semantics for internships (salary stays annual to avoid a seeker-side change), the Hot Vacancy/SMB per-type field differences (those products are gated OFF), and an edit-existing-job UI (PATCH exists, no screen consumes it).

### `feature/recruiter-post-a-job` — Phase 2 (template picker + job-type selector) · 2026-07-09

**Post a Job revamp — Phase 2** (SRS §4.9). CLI merge to `develop` (`--no-ff`). Recruiter-only, **no schema/migration**; `apps/web` + `apps/services` **byte-untouched** (`git diff develop -- apps/web apps/services` empty). Adds the pre-wizard flow (a template step + a job-type product selector) in front of the existing wizard; the rich form redesign + persistence still come in Phase 3.

**What shipped:**
- **`PostJobFlow`** (new client orchestrator) wraps `PostJobWizard`: **Start** → *Create a new job* → **Job-type selector** → form · **or** → *Start from a previous job* → **Template picker** → form. Holds the chosen job type + any template prefill; remounts the wizard (keyed) per selection so state re-seeds cleanly. A "Start over" header + a "copied from a previous job" banner (template path) sit above the wizard.
- **`JobTypeSelector`** — four product cards (Free Job / Hot Vacancy / SMB Pack / Internship), Naukri-informed and restyled to our minimal look (bordered cards, one accent on Free, no badge-shouting). **Free + Internship are always selectable; Hot Vacancy + SMB render locked ("Upgrade")** until their flags flip ON. Definitions/field-intent captured in a new `apps/recruiter/lib/job-types.ts`.
- **`TemplatePicker`** (Indeed-style "start from a previous job") — a searchable list of the recruiter's **own** past postings, filterable by **Status + Location** (options derived from what they actually have); "Use as template" fetches the full job via `GET /recruiter/jobs/:id` and deep-copies it into the form. Empty-state + per-row loading handled.
- **`PostJobWizard`** gains optional `initialValues` (template prefill; salary paise→LPA converted, `key`-remount avoids stale state) + `jobType` (Internship presets employment type to **INTERN**; display label otherwise). **`jobType` is UI-only this phase** — deliberately NOT sent to the `.strict()` `POST /recruiter/jobs` DTO, since there is no `Job.jobType` column until Phase 3.
- **`/post-job` RSC** now also fetches the recruiter's past jobs (lightweight list, IST-formatted `postedLabel`) + the two new flag states, and renders `PostJobFlow`.
- **Flags:** `recruiter.hot_vacancy.enabled` + `recruiter.smb_pack.enabled` (BOOLEAN, category `recruiter`, seeded **OFF** per CLAUDE.md §0) added to `keys.ts` + `seed/flags.ts` (now **38 flags**). These are the UI/product gates; the trusted per-type API enforcement lands with Phase 3.

The Internship→INTERN preset is typechecked + shares the verified Free handoff but wasn't driven to the employment-type wizard step live (reaching it needs skills/city filled).
